# Gravity Arc — Tiered-Claims Ledger

**What this is.** Every load-bearing claim in the gravity folder (the corpus flagship) and its *current* tier. Three things at once: (1) the **tier catalog**, (2) the **staleness detector** (the `Status` column is current state; any doc disagreeing with a row is by definition stale), (3) the **anti-drift authority** `read-first` checks against.

**How it's built.** Read every paper; tier each claim *from the paper's own audit table / "does NOT claim" / verdict* — never inflated. Seeded from the folder README + `event-density/docs/ED_Research_Targets.md` (#1, #5, #5b), corrected against the papers where they disagree.

**One of a per-folder set.** Companions: `entanglement/…`, `black-hole/…`, `cosmology/…`, `dark-sector/…`. This folder *synthesizes and stands* (it holds the standing KM-I MOND result — do not reinvent it).

**Tier key.** `Derived` (from primitives/structure, conditioning postulate noted) · `Grounded` (structural/conditional/account-tier) · `Measured` (simulation/probe) · `Selected/Inherited` (a value: G, ℓ_P, a₀, Λ-magnitude) · `Postulated` · `Asserted` (A→position) · `Prediction` · `Synthesis` · `Open`. `D-cond` = derived conditional on a named postulate.

*~30 papers. Read 2026-07-29 (extraction agent, then spot-checked); scope items propagated 2026-09-03 (see Staleness #9); the a₀ `1/(2π)` re-tiered to disputed 2026-09-04 (see Staleness #10). Honest-arc-state paragraph reconciled with Staleness #1 on 2026-08-30: it had continued to describe the a₀(z) propagation as the folder's urgent open task after Staleness #1 recorded that propagation as DONE on 2026-07-29 — the ledger contradicted itself on its own headline debt. No claim tier changed.*
*Spot-checked directly against the papers (7, scaled to the 30-paper flagship; **no catch** — the extraction was reliable throughout): (1) **GR-IV** α₂=0 exact + the c_T=c/c_s=c causal-cone framing + literature-verification — confirmed verbatim (§3, boxed); (2) **029** a₀=cH₀/(2π) form-derived, H₀ inherited, and the STATIC "tracks whichever H₀" reading (no a₀(z)) — confirmed (§8.5); (3) **031 §8.8** carries the MUSE-DARK III (A&A 2026) ~30σ a₀(z) confirmation — confirmed verbatim; (4) **038.5 §3.5** "updated 2026-07-16," w=−1 resolved, Λ=frozen floor, force-vs-stipulate residual — confirmed (this makes the MEMORY pointer stale, not §3.5); (5) **MetricFromGraph** metric KINEMATIC/static-linear, nonlinear OPEN, exactly-3 not a forcing — confirmed (preamble #2, §5); (6) **027** G=c³ℓ_P²/ħ downstream identification, ℓ_P value-layer — confirmed; (7) **QuadraticStrain** discharges P14 (geometric mean = forced interference modulus) + the regime assumption, residual = constructive sign — confirmed.*

> **Session-boundary note, added 2026-09-05.** The working session that produced items **#10 through #57** ran continuously across midnight. Items **#10–#45 are 2026-09-04**; items **#46–#57 are 2026-09-05** — their headers are corrected, but **body text and the dated flags those items wrote into papers still say `2026-09-04`**, because they were written before the boundary was noticed. **Read any `2026-09-04` stamp inside items #46–#57, or in the flags they placed, as “this session” rather than as a precise date.** Git is authoritative: `git log --format='%ad %s' --date=short` on this branch separates them exactly. 147. **THE `a₀(z)` STANDALONE PAPER DRAFTED, THEN ADVERSARIALLY REVIEWED — and the review found one objection that may be fatal to its headline. 2026-09-06.** AP's call: write the one paper a physicist could evaluate in an afternoon, then attack it. `gravity/Paper_a0z_MONDScaleTracksHubbleRate.md` + `gravity/Note_a0z_Paper_AdversarialReview_2026-09-06.md`.

    **The paper.** One claim, self-contained, ~16pp: `a₀(z) = cH(z)/(2π)` with the exponent as the tested content. **Its structural move is to separate the disputed coefficient from the tested exponent** — measuring the SHAPE of `a₀(z)` tests `α` and is independent of `K` entirely, so a reader sceptical of the `2π` (and there is reason to be) can set it aside. **Numbers, from the corpus's own prediction list rather than memory:** MUSE-DARK III detects evolution at **~30σ**; constant `a₀` excluded at **29σ**; fitted **`α = 1.18 ± 0.04` (stat)**, putting `α = 1` at **+4.4σ**, softened to ~1–2σ on systematics; local intercept matches to **~8%**. **Kill condition stated in advance: a multi-survey fit returning `α` inconsistent with 1 at ≥3σ refutes it.**

    **The review found three things the drafting did not.** **(1) POSSIBLY FATAL — “MOND excluded at 29σ” is not a claim about MOND.** The draft's own §1.1 concedes the `a₀–cH₀` connection is **standard MOND material** (Milgrom 2020 is a review of it; the corpus corrected a “no MOND analogue” claim as FALSE on 2026-09-05) — and then §1.2's three-way table **quietly assumes constant-`a₀` MOND is the only MOND. The paper contradicts itself across one page.** **Worse: if MOND admits a FITTED exponent, it fits `α = 1.18` exactly while this prediction sits 4.4σ off — so on its own evidence the data currently favour MOND-with-a-free-exponent over ED.** The untunable `α` is a virtue **only when it is right**. **(2) SERIOUS — the 8% local match may be CIRCULAR:** §3.2 admits the `2π` is reinstated by a normalisation *“set to the value that returns the result”*, so offering the local match as evidence in §5.2 reports the calibration as a result. **(3) SERIOUS — the coefficient failure undermines confidence in the exponent:** both come from the same mechanism, and that mechanism demonstrably failed to deliver `K`. Formal independence is not evidential independence. **There may be a good answer** — the exponent is a counting statement (powers of `H` per live horizon), the coefficient depends on the angular integrals where the failure occurred — **and the paper does not make it.**

    **Also flagged: step 9 is special pleading without a number** (arguing the published `±0.04` is too small while producing no alternative budget, against both the number and the survey team's own “faster than `H(z)`” reading); the evidence base is **one survey with unpublished per-bin data**; and §7 claims to stand alone while naming four corpus papers as its chain. **And the paper inherits a live corpus contradiction it annotates rather than fixes: the prediction list says `α = 1` is DIMENSIONALLY forced, the report says it is NOT (because `ℓ_P` exists). The report's careful version is used; the source disagreement should be fixed at source.**

    **Recommendation from the review: rewrite as “a registered prediction UNDER TENSION”** — lead with the tension, treat the 30σ as context, make the paper about a sharp unfudgeable exponent and the test that settles it. **Weaker headline, much harder to dismiss.** **What survives regardless: the prediction was registered before the data with `×1.8 at z=1` explicit, the exponent is genuinely untunable, evolution WAS detected, and the kill condition is stated in advance** — which most theory papers do not do. **Review limitation recorded in the review itself: same session drafted and reviewed, which is exactly the arrangement `CLAUDE.md`'s review rule exists to prevent; it lowers the cost of an outside read, it does not replace one.** Census unchanged at 174.

142. **EXTERNAL REVIEW OF THE REPORT ANSWERED — three of four points were ALREADY SETTLED IN THE CORPUS, the fourth is right for a different reason, and §5 now carries all of it. 2026-09-06.** An outside reader raised four points against the report's §5. **Checked against the ledgers rather than accepted or dismissed.**

    **(1) The MOND sign mechanism.** The reader argued §5 describes `Str = D + X` with `Coh` inert, which they computed gives a **repulsive** MOND term, and that only *diagonal in `Str`, cross-term in `Coh`* works. **That is foundations ledger #10, settled 2026-09-05** — same conclusion, same reasoning (*“the Newtonian piece goes as `1/R`, the MOND piece as `ln R` … both attractive requires opposite signs, which one squared modulus cannot supply”*), settled by a **three-way sign check** in which exactly one assignment reproduces `a = a_N + √(a_N a₀)` to floating-point precision at every radius (`internal notes/_check_sigma_sign.py`). **So an independent reader re-derived the corpus's own result a day later — a genuine convergence.** **Their actionable half stands: the REPORT never said which term the cross-term lives in.** Now it does.

    **(2) The ‘forced, not chosen’ √.** The reader argued `b^{(L)}b^{(H)} = GMa₀` is **R-independent**, so there is no `R`-dependence to make a force, and called the aggregation step missing. **The specific objection is WRONG:** the cross-term enters as `√(GMa₀)·ln(R/R₀)` and its gradient is `√(GMa₀)/R = √(a_N a₀)` — the `R`-dependence is in the **logarithm**, which is why the sign check reproduces the target at every radius. **And P14 IS genuinely discharged** (§5.2: the geometric mean is the unavoidable off-diagonal of `|P^(L)+P^(H)|²`, while the alternatives Paper_030 could not exclude are the *diagonal* self-terms — a different part of the same expansion), **forced GIVEN `P-Quadratic-Strain`, which is exactly the `Grounded` tier the report assigned.** **BUT THEY WERE RIGHT THAT SOMETHING IS OVERSTATED, for a reason they did not have:** the arc **withdrew** its companion claim that the *regime assumption* was also discharged — a third branch was tried and **breaks MOND** (`a ∝ GM H₀/R²`), so `Paper_030` §5.3's regime assumption is **restored** and *“two postulates to one”* reads **one and a half** (Staleness #13). **And that withdrawal surfaced what the arc calls arguably its deepest open question in the MOND account: `Paper_029` gives a horizon response first-order in `|a|` while MOND requires a THRESHOLD at `a₀` — how does a linear response produce a threshold? Neither paper asks it.** **None of this was in the report.** Now it is.

    **(3) ‘Split the sign residual in two.’ INVERTED.** #10 makes the residual **narrower**, not broader: under the settled assignment `Coh` enters with a **plus**, so `cos Θ_LH > 0` follows **structurally** — a second support independent of any simulation. **But the reader's spreadsheet pass separately caught the real weakness, and correctly:** #112 found the *Measured* sign run implements `|acc|/n`, which has no neighbour-to-neighbour term, so **it measured `Grad`'s phase behaviour, not `Coh`'s**. **So the residual is structurally narrower and empirically weaker at the same time, and §5 now says both.**

    **(4) ‘The P12 / universal-free-fall tension is nowhere in the corpus.’ IT IS — and further along than the reader had it.** `Foundations_TieredClaims_Ledger.md` **#9, dated 2026-09-05**, reached **independently by two of three external runs**, with the same derivation (`√b_K` in the chain's own bandwidth, `a_C = −∇_adj Σ_C` carrying no inertial normalisation). **The corpus then CHECKED it and weakened it from *contradiction* to *unproven*:** `Paper_MassWithoutMass_BindingInertia` **measures inertia natively** — a bound composite under uniform force reaches `v_x = 0.72` against an unbound cluster's `0.97`, so **ED has an inertia mechanism**; what it lacks is a demonstration that it CANCELS from gravitational acceleration. Cross-filed as Staleness #69. **Not added to §5 here — it belongs where universal free fall is claimed, not in the MOND passage, and that is a separate placement call.**

    **The pattern, again: the corpus knew and the REPORT lagged** — the same shape as #126's a₀ propagation and #136's A1 citation. **Three of the four answers existed, dated, with scripts, before the review arrived.** §5 now names the `Coh`/`Str` split, carries the regime-assumption withdrawal and the linear-response-versus-threshold open, reports the `KM-I`/`Paper_030` high-acceleration tension (#19) that its *“standing MOND result”* framing had omitted, and states the constructive sign at **candidate** tier. PDF rebuilt. Census unchanged at 174.

140. **NAVIGATION FILES FIXED — 0 dead live pointers; AND #138's HEADLINE NUMBER WAS WRONG, which is the more useful half. 2026-09-06.** **The correction first.** #138 reported *“~111 genuinely missing, almost all pre-existing and **concentrated in the navigation files** … which matters because those are the read-first entry points”*. **That was an over-statement produced by a crude checker, and the triage says so.** Run against git history, the four navigation files held **27 dead names, of which only SIX were real defects.** **The rest are a deliberate REMOVED-FILES PROVENANCE TABLE that `PAPERS_INDEX.md` maintains and maintains well** — rows like *“`Paper_008_KernelArrow.md` | **Renamed, not archived.** Git shows a single-file rename-plus-edit into its successor … the content *became* Paper_093”*, and *“`primitives/Primitives_Ledger.md` → `P1-13_Primitives_Ledger.md` | renamed. **Live pointer in this index repointed 2026-09-03**”*. **Naming the dead file THERE is the point, and ‘fixing’ those lines would have destroyed the record.** **The index is in better shape than I said, and it already does the job I was proposing to do for it.**

    **And the checker was rebuilt, because the first version's number was the problem.** `_check_doc_coherence.py` now (a) **skips PROVENANCE lines** — a filename on a line carrying a strike-through or a death-word is a record, not a pointer — and (b) **indexes the SIBLING REPOS**, since this repo legitimately cites `event-density`/`ed-lab` files by bare name. **The number that matters is now separated from the noise: NAVIGATION FILES 0, elsewhere 81, resolved-in-a-sibling 407 (not defects).** **The 81 are mostly old `domain-arcs/` paths and `project_*.md` memory files inside individual papers — real but low-traffic, and not the read-first path. Not fixed here.**

139. **`P-Channel-Orthogonality` BEARS ON THE BRANCH QUESTION — its RESOLUTION does, and it makes the seam sharper: BINARY vs GRADED. 2026-09-06.** **Its status is not ‘blocking’.** `Paper_004`'s *“fully open / three routes fail”* is stale by that paper's own update box (and today's C2 pass re-tiered the sheet row `Open → Superseded`). The current word is the Gleason reconstruction §4: *“**ED's channels are perfectly distinguishable**; perfectly distinguishable ⇺ orthogonal; therefore `⟨K|L⟩ = 0` is **not an independent postulate** … **P07 distinctness gives `p(K|L) = 0`**”*. **Orthogonality is REDUCED, and the operative fact it reduces to is perfect channel distinguishability.**

    **AND THAT FACT IS THE PROBLEM.** The collapse-rate derivation needs distinguishability to be something branches **acquire over time** — *“individuates them **as soon as they become** commitment-distinguishable … `E_G τ ~ ℏ`”*. **But if branches are channels they are perfectly distinguishable from the OUTSET:** `p(K|L) = 0` is a standing structural fact from P07, not a quantity that accumulates. **So if branches were channels, `τ` would be ZERO and collapse instantaneous — contradicting `τ ∝ ℏ/E_G`, the arc's headline.** **The derivation therefore REQUIRES that its branches are not channels** — which is #137's layer seam **reached from the opposite end**: #137 argued it from what the substrate represents, this argues it from what P07 already guarantees. **Two independent routes, same conclusion.**

    **AND IT SHARPENS THE GAP INTO SOMETHING NAMEABLE: BINARY vs GRADED.** **Sense 1 (commitment / channels) is BINARY** — P07 gives `p(K|L) = 0`, perfect and standing. **Sense 2 (the system/environment cut) is GRADED** — `R = b_int/b_bdry` is continuous, and the concept card says so outright: *“Fully individuated and fully non-individuated are limits; **most real systems sit somewhere on the continuum**”*. **The collapse derivation needs a GRADED distinguishability crossing a threshold as `τ` accumulates — only sense 2 is graded — while the event that does the collapsing, commitment, is sense 1 and BINARY.** **So the missing bridge is not merely ‘a bridge between two senses’; it is a bridge from a GRADED quantity to a BINARY one — and THAT is what a coefficient would be: the conversion factor between how far the graded quantity has run and when the binary event fires.** **Which is why the coefficient is missing:** #136 showed A1 cannot supply it, #137 showed the object it would apply to does not exist at the substrate layer, **and this says what KIND of thing it would have to be and why neither sense supplies it alone.**

    **What is NOT touched.** **The Gleason reconstruction is not challenged** — its reduction of orthogonality to P07 distinctness stands, the Solèr residual stands, **and this note DEPENDS on that reduction being right.** **The collapse arc is not falsified** — `τ ∝ ℏ/E_G` remains grounded-in-kind and the derivation's own C4 flag already says the energy is *identified*, not computed; **this explains why C4 could never have been discharged by working harder at the integral.** **No bridge is proposed here.**

    **The one thing now newly answerable:** *“what would the collapse coefficient be a coefficient OF?”* — **the rate at which a graded, locus-side quantity drives a binary, channel-side event.** **A well-posed question, in a way ‘push the A1 machinery to a coefficient’ was not.** **Still not a small one:** it needs the graded quantity named, and `R` is the corpus's only graded individuation measure — **but #135 put `θ_ind` 25 orders of magnitude from the operative scale, so if the graded quantity is `R`, something must carry it up 25 orders and nothing does.** **Open, and now open in a STATED FORM rather than as a gap.** `state-reduction/Note_Orthogonality_BinaryVsGraded_2026-09-06.md`. Census unchanged at 174.

137. **THE BRANCH-REPRESENTATION QUESTION — ED's superposition is over CHANNELS, the collapse derivation needs superposition over PLACES, and that layer seam is why the coefficient is missing. 2026-09-06.** **ED does have a branch representation:** `Paper_001` §4.1's `Ψ^C(u,t) = Σ_K P_K^C(u,t)|K⟩`, with §4.2 saying outright that this is *“the substrate-level analog of quantum superposition — a chain's participation distributed across **multiple channels**”*. **The index is `ℬ`. It is NOT the locus set.** **And it is conditional:** `Paper_007`'s audit tiers the motif algebra **P (`P-Motif-Algebra`, definitional)**, the inner product **I** (*“not derived in this paper”*, still blocked on `P-Channel-Orthogonality`), and the Hilbert space **D conditional on both** — so the space that carries superposition rests on a definitional postulate plus an inherited inner product whose blocker is open.

    **AND THE CERTIFIED SUBSTRATE DOES NOT BRANCH — MEASURED.** `Paper_Continuum_KineticLatticeGas` §3, tier **measured**, *“direct observation”*: *“**The certified front does not branch.** One seed → exactly one active front after many steps, depositing `ρ` along a 1-D chain (a worldline). The Σ-rule is a **chain propagator, not a field-update rule**.”* **So #136's proposed A1-style branch experiment CANNOT be run on the certified substrate as a matter of MEASURED FACT, not of design difficulty — which is stronger than #136 said. There is no branch structure in the simulator to intervene on;** it models the commitment dynamics, not the pre-commitment amplitude structure. **#136's redirect is re-scoped accordingly.**

    **THE FINDING IS A LAYER SEAM.** The collapse-rate paper opens *“put a mass in a superposition of two configurations … each sources its own bandwidth field `b₁(x)`, `b₂(x)`”* — **fields over space; the branches are two MASS CONFIGURATIONS.** But the threshold that fires is substrate-layer: commitment (P11) selects **a channel at a locus**. **Two mass configurations indexed by continuum `x` versus two channels indexed by `ℬ` at one locus — different objects, different layers, and the bridge is not stated.** **This IS the arc's own C4 flag, named as what it is:** C4 says the step *“**identifies** the branch-clock-difference energy with the known Penrose `E_G` rather than **computing** it from the ED participation fields”*, **and that identification is precisely the layer crossing.** **So C4 is not a missing integral someone could sit down and do — it is a missing statement of WHAT TWO SPATIAL CONFIGURATIONS ARE, as substrate channel content.** **Which is why the coefficient is missing for a STRUCTURAL reason, not a measurement one:** #136 showed A1 cannot supply it; this says what would, and that **until a substrate-layer spatial branch exists there is no object for a distinguishability threshold to be evaluated on, hence nothing for a coefficient to be a coefficient OF.**

    **A candidate bridge, offered as a SKETCH and explicitly NOT BANKED.** `P-Commitment-Advancement` (#117) is the corpus's only channel→locus bridge, so **channel choice generates locus displacement** and two channel-histories differing in which propagation-carrying channels committed end in different places — the shape a substrate account of spatial branching would have. **But it does not close, and the tension is worth naming: advancement happens AT commitment, so an uncommitted chain sits at ONE locus with multi-channel content, while a lump in two places needs two spatial configurations, which needs commitments to have already happened — yet the branches must be UNCOMMITTED to be superposed at all.** **The consistent reading is that spatial superposition is NOT a single-chain substrate object** but a coarse-grained, many-chain **continuum** description: each chain has a definite locus and `b(x)` is the ensemble object. **Consistent with everything the corpus already says** — *“unitarity emergent, not fundamental”*, *“between commitments = reversible QM”*, and the measured non-branching front. **Tier: a sketch of where a bridge would have to live, not a bridge. Nothing downstream should cite it as one.**

    **NOTHING IN ANY TIER MOVES.** The escape stands, `τ ∝ ℏ/E_G` stands as grounded-in-kind, locality stands. **What changes is the SHAPE of two open items:** the collapse coefficient is not awaiting a measurement or a harder A1 run but **a substrate-layer representation of a spatial branch**, which does not exist and which the certified substrate **cannot exhibit**; and #136's branch experiment is re-scoped as blocked for a measured reason. **Stated plainly: ED's substrate represents superposition over CHANNELS and the collapse derivation needs superposition over PLACES — different objects at different layers, with one postulate pointing at the seam and no construction across it.** `state-reduction/Note_BranchRepresentation_2026-09-06.md`. Census unchanged at 174.
136. **A1 ANSWERS A DIFFERENT QUESTION — it measures REGION-to-REGION transmission, not BRANCH distinguishability, so two residuals' plan of attack is void. 2026-09-06.** **What survives first, because it is most of it.** **The 2020-bound escape stands exactly as written** — its load-bearing step (Lindblad jump operators are channel projectors `Ĵ_α = g_α Π̂_α`, so committed charges are exact fixed points) is **FORCED from P11 and never touches A1**. **The locality advantage stands and its A1 citation is CORRECT:** *“controlled inter-region capacity = exactly zero (A1, measured)”* is precisely what A1 found, used for precisely what it supports. **And `ChannelGranularity` Pillars 1 and 2 need no A1 at all** — `ℓ_ED`-fine dynamics is coherent Schrödinger evolution, and the pointer basis is environment-selected einselection — **so between them they already carry the conclusion that the pointer basis is coarse.**

    **The problem is narrow and lives in the RESIDUALS.** `CollapseRate`: *“the exact prefactor … rides on the precise distinguishability threshold … **Rigor here = the same distinguishability/A1 machinery, pushed to a coefficient**”*. `ChannelGranularity` Pillar 3: *“**the A1 result measures it as controlled channel capacity** … Operational distinguishability has a coarse floor”*. **A1 cannot be pushed to that coefficient, for two independent reasons.**

    **(a) IT MEASURES A DIFFERENT PAIR OF THINGS.** A1's experiment is **region-to-region**: encode into region A's left half, try to decode from A's right half or from region B. **The objects are two spatial regions.** But branch distinguishability — *“the arrow individuates two branches exactly when they become operationally distinguishable”* — has as its objects **branch 1 and branch 2 of a superposition**. **Whether region X can signal region Y and whether branch 1 can be told from branch 2 are not the same question, and A1 only ran the first.** **Pillar 3 imports A1's SPIRIT** — distinguishability is operational rather than substrate-omniscient, which is fair — **but not a measurement of the relevant quantity, because none was made.**

    **(b) AND A1'S OWN VERDICT FORECLOSES THE PLAN.** Its measured value is **exactly zero, everywhere** (*“within a stratum and across the boundary alike”*). **If distinguishability simply WERE controlled channel capacity it would be zero everywhere and commitment could never individuate anything** — a reductio showing the identification cannot be literal. And A1 states its limit outright: *“capacity does not yield a positive intrinsic determinability number; it is zero everywhere … **there is no canonical positive determinability scalar to be found this way**”*, *“what is observable-invariant about the boundary is **a zero, not a scalar**”*. **BOTH BRANCHES OF THE BITS FORK ARE CLOSED for an intrinsic positive number:** observational `Δ` is **positive but observable-DEPENDENT** (*“the scalar `Δ` is not intrinsic”*); interventional capacity is **intrinsic but exactly ZERO**. **That fork was run and reported in June 2026; the state-reduction papers were written in July 2026 and point at it for a number it had already concluded is not there.** Ordinary cross-arc staleness — **and it costs the arc nothing except the plan.**

    **The redirect, and it is concrete.** **The experiment that would settle it is an A1-style interventional run on two BRANCHES rather than two REGIONS** — encode into branch-distinguishing structure, evolve, ask at what separation commitment resolves them. **It has never been run.** **Honest feasibility caveat: the certified substrate is DETERMINATE** — it commits, it does not carry superposed branches — **so this is not `capacity.py` with different arguments; it needs a construction representing two branches on a determinate substrate, which is a design question, not a parameter change. Naming it is not the same as having it.** **Meanwhile the floor's operative value stays environment-set**, where residual 2 already put it and where #135 independently pushed it from the locus side.

    **What to change is small: `CollapseRate`'s coefficient residual should stop saying “the same A1 machinery, pushed to a coefficient”** (that machinery is region-to-region and its output is zero); **`ChannelGranularity` Pillar 3 should keep its conclusion and soften its warrant** (*“distinguishability is operational”* is supported by Report §4 and A1's framing; *“A1 measures it”* is not supported by what A1 measured — **and the pillar's conclusion is carried by Pillars 1 and 2 regardless**). **NO TIER MOVES, NO CLAIM WEAKENS.** Flagged in place at both residuals. `state-reduction/Note_A1_IsNotTheFloorMachinery_2026-09-06.md`. Census unchanged at 174.
135. **PATH E OPENED — and its first move is a BANKED NEGATIVE plus a CORRECTION TO ME. 2026-09-06.** **First the correction.** I described the channel-sense collapse account as having *“no paper, only a rate in `Paper_054` §3.4”*. **That was WRONG, and wrong in the way `CLAUDE.md` exists to prevent — a gap claimed without searching.** **The `state-reduction/` arc is FOUR substantive papers plus a summary, refereed twice and self-tiered**, carrying a collapse-rate derivation (`τ ∝ ℏ/E_G`, *Grounded-in-kind*, `E_G` honestly flagged identified-not-computed), a pointer-basis account (*Reconstruction*, Solèr the standing residual), a channel-granularity *Structural result* (Lindblad jump operators are channel projectors; committed charges are exact fixed points), and the **arrow = gravity's time = pointer-basis selector** reframe with its *“distinctive, structural, unconfirmed”* guardrail carried verbatim. **The account exists and is in better shape than I represented.**

    **What Path E actually is:** `StateReduction_ChannelGranularity.md` residual 1 — *“locating the floor precisely — **is it environment-set, or is there an intrinsic ED distinguishability floor above `ℓ_ED`?**”* **`θ_ind = 1` looks like it answers that either/or**: a substrate-intrinsic, environment-free length saying **no locus set below 3 `ℓ_ED` in linear extent is individuated at all**.

    **IT DOES NOT, AND THE REASON IS TODAY'S OWN DISAMBIGUATION.** The tempting argument — *a branch's displaced region must itself be individuated for two branches to be distinguishable, so the pointer basis cannot be finer than the minimal individuated region* — **crosses the two senses this session spent the day separating:** *“region `S` is not individuated”* is **sense 2** (a locus-set criterion), *“so commitment cannot individuate those branches”* is **sense 1** (a channel event). **The ONLY bridge the corpus has is `P-Commitment-Advancement`, and that postulate is about whether a commitment ADVANCES THE LOCUS — it says nothing about distinguishability.** **So the inference needs a bridge that does not exist, and supplying it would be a new postulate or theorem, not a step to slip in.** **`θ_ind` does not answer residual 1 and is not close to it.**

    **AND IT WOULD NOT MATTER IF IT DID.** `ℓ_ED = ℓ_P ≈ 1.6×10⁻³⁵ m`, so the candidate intrinsic floor is **`3ℓ_ED ≈ 4.8×10⁻³⁵ m`** against the scale the emission escape actually needs — an ordinary bound charge at **Å–nm, `~10⁻¹⁰ m`**. **That is ~25 ORDERS OF MAGNITUDE. Even granted the bridge, an intrinsic floor at 3 `ℓ_ED` does none of the work.** **The coarse pointer basis must stay environment-set** — which is what the arc already says, and this makes that dependence **essential rather than incidental**: residual 2's scoping (*“not a universal guarantee for every conceivable environment, only for the ones these experiments actually sit in”*) **is not a gap awaiting an intrinsic floor. It is the right shape of the claim.**

    **What it is worth: residual 1's two horns are NOT SYMMETRIC.** The **environment-set** floor is operative, `≫ ℓ_ED`, and is what the escape rests on; the **intrinsic** floor plausibly exists, is unreachable from `θ_ind` without a new bridge, and is **25 orders too small to be the operative one even if reached**. **So the remaining constructive work is entirely on the A1 / channel-capacity side** — quantifying the *operational* distinguishability floor set by what the available channels resolve. **`θ_ind` and the locus-side machinery are not the tool for it, and an hour spent making them fit would have been an hour wasted.** **NOTHING IN THE ARC CHANGES: no tier moves, no claim weakens, the escape stands as it stood.** What is recorded is that **one tempting route into residual 1 is closed, with the reason and the number.** `state-reduction/Note_PathE_IntrinsicFloor_2026-09-06.md`. Census unchanged at 174.
134. **§5.1's WORDING FIX APPLIED on instruction. 2026-09-06.** #133 settled the reading and proposed the substitution; AP instructed applying it. **`Paper_072`'s own text: `rule-type assignments` → `channel assignments` (×4), `rule-type assignment` → `channel assignment` (×1), `multi-rule-type coexistence` → `multi-channel coexistence` (×1).** **The word “channel” now appears 14 times in a paper that contained it ZERO times**, so the index is stated rather than inferred. **The regime's own name, “participation-rule individuation”, is LEFT AS IS** — it names the regime, not the index, and renaming it would retitle the paper; the index note says to read it as the individuation of the participation **configuration** over channels. **The index note's own paragraph quotes the pre-substitution phrasing deliberately and was carved out of the substitution** — a first pass would have rewritten the explanation into nonsense, and the safe-edit assert caught it before any write. **No tier changes, no structural changes; `Paper_072` remains `Asserted` / `A→position`.** Census unchanged at 174.
133. **§5.1 SETTLED — `Paper_072`'s “rule-type” is WORDING, not a third index set, and one of its two readings breaks the paper's own thesis. 2026-09-06.** #132 left this as the one item needing the author. **Settled by two facts and a test.**

    **(i) Rule-type is a COARSER partition of the SAME index set.** Branch 3 established that classifying `𝒦(u)` by rule-type *“reads a label the substrate already carries”* — **channels CARRY rule-type labels**, so selecting a channel fixes a rule-type and **not conversely**. **Canonical P11 selects the finer thing:** *“collapses to single-channel participation”*. **(ii) `Paper_072` uses “rule-type” FIVE times and the word “channel” ZERO times** — while citing `Paper_087` §P11 and `Paper_054` (**both channel-indexed**) and sitting on `Papers_063–068`, whose machinery is `Ψ_KL^AB` on `𝒦_A × 𝒦_B`. **It is the only paper in its own chain to use the word.** **A paper citing two channel-indexed sources, on channel-indexed machinery, using neither the word nor the index, is doing vocabulary rather than physics.**

    **The test that settles it: two readings, and only one survives.** **(a) The LABEL reading** — *assignment* = the rule-type the selected channel carries, so *“multiple assignments coexist”* means two or more **rule-types** are live. **THIS BREAKS THE PAPER'S OWN THESIS.** A bipartite entangled state is a superposition over channel **pairs** (`Papers_063/064`, Schmidt rank > 1), and **nothing requires those channels to differ in rule-type — a two-qubit entangled state is matter rule-type throughout.** **So under (a) a standard entangled state has a unique rule-type assignment and is NOT in the unresolved regime — which is exactly what `Paper_072` exists to say it IS.** **(b) The CONFIGURATION reading** — *assignment* = the whole map of participation across channels, so multiple assignments = several participation **configurations** simultaneously supported. **Equivalent to the channel statement and consistent with P11, `Paper_054` and the arc's machinery.** **Reading (b) is the only one consistent with the paper's claim, so it is what is meant.**

    **Verdict: no third index set, no third sense. `INDIVIDUATION_DISAMBIGUATION.md` stays a TWO-sense document, `Paper_072` stays `Asserted` (`A→position`), and the entanglement arc is untouched.** **What is owed is a small wording fix** — five instances of *“rule-type assignment”* → *“channel assignment”*, plus one use of “channel” so the index is stated once — **PROPOSED, NOT APPLIED**, on the same convention as #36/#37, since it narrows a published paper in a nominally closed arc.

    **And one thing the check earned beyond the answer.** `Paper_072`'s abstract says the identification *“composes: (i) UR-1 (`Paper_054`) … (ii) V5 (`Paper_090`) … (iii) P11”*. **But (i) and (iii) are NOT independent inputs:** #132 established that `Paper_054` §3.4's `Γ_individuation` **IS the rate of P11, in P11's own words.** **So a composition of THREE is claimed where TWO are the same primitive.** **This does not make the identification wrong — it is an `A→position` either way — but the support is two-part, not three-part**, and that is now recorded where the paper claims it. Boxed in place at `Paper_072` §2 (index note) and at the abstract's composition claim. `foundations/Note_Paper072_RuleTypeOrChannel_2026-09-06.md`. Census unchanged at 174.
132. **THE INDIVIDUATION DISAMBIGUATION IS WRITTEN — and the sweep found the footprint is 616 occurrences across 81 files. 2026-09-06.** `primitives/INDIVIDUATION_DISAMBIGUATION.md`, on the `ADJACENCY_AND_BAND_DISAMBIGUATION.md` template, with `internal notes/_check_individuation_senses.py` so the spread stays **measured rather than asserted**.

    **THE SHARPEST THING IN IT: sense 1 IS P11, verbatim.** Canonical `Paper_087` §P11 reads *“a chain's multi-channel participation **collapses to single-channel participation**”*; `q-compute/Paper_054` §3.4 defines `Γ_individuation` as *“the substrate's primitive timescale for **resolving multi-channel participation into single-channel participation**”*. **Those are the same sentence. “Individuation” in sense 1 is a SECOND NAME FOR COMMITMENT, and that redundancy is what made the collision possible.** Sense 2 — the system/environment cut, `b_int/b_bdry > θ_ind = 1` — ranges over **loci**, is a dimensionless **ratio**, and measures a **linear extent** (#128).

    **AND THE TWO SENSES ARE ANTI-CORRELATED, which is why this bites rather than merely being untidy.** Raise a system's boundary bandwidth to an external device: **sense 1 goes UP** (coupling drives commitment) while **sense 2 goes DOWN** (`b_bdry` up ⇒ `R` down). **That is exactly why the source card's §5.3 and §5.4 describe the same substrate change as *“individuation is sharp”* and *“individuation weakens”* in consecutive paragraphs — neither is wrong, they answer different questions.** **And the split is the one `P-Commitment-Advancement` (#117) had to bridge: P11 selects a CHANNEL and is silent on the LOCUS. Sense 1 is the channel half, sense 2 the locus half, and the postulate is the ONLY bridge between them** — which is also why `θ_ind` and the Class-A wall are independent (#131).

    **MEASURED: 616 occurrences, 81 files — 55% sense 1, 14% sense 2, 32% AMBIGUOUS** (no marker either way, or both vocabularies in one window). **A footprint comparable to the four-band overload, and the ambiguous third is the work list — not errors, but places where the sense must be guessed.** **The pure-sense-1 files are clean and unambiguous:** `Paper_054` **46/0/0**, `Paper_001` **35/0/2**, `Paper_072` **13/0/0**. **The most mixed is the source card itself** (**8/45/53**) — exactly as predicted, since it is the one document carrying both. **Sense 1 dominating is EXPECTED and is not a defect:** sense 1 is P11, which the corpus has always had; sense 2 has one paper, promoted 2026-09-05.

    **Three inconsistencies recorded, none resolved unilaterally.** **(5.1)** `Paper_072` says *“rule-type assignments”* where canonical P11 collapses to a single **`K`, a channel** — whether that is a synonym or a different index **is not stated**; same shape as the four-band *location-not-existence* finding, **and it needs AP.** **(5.2)** `Paper_ED_Inflation`'s *“P12 ED-threshold”* is a **different threshold from `θ_ind`**, which is also P12-adjacent and now fixed at 1 — **flagged so the two numbers are not merged by someone reading both.** **(5.3)** the card's *“best cut maximises `b_int/b_bdry`”* is **vacuous** under union closure — corrected in place.

    **Writing rule, §4: if the object is a CHANNEL, say commitment; if it is a set of LOCI, say cut.** **Explicitly NOT claimed:** that any arc's physics is wrong (all internally sound — **the word is what is overloaded**); that the senses should be unified; **or that sense 1 should be renamed corpus-wide** — §4 is a rule for NEW text, and retitling `Γ_individuation` or `Paper_072` is AP's call since each is cited downstream. Pointers wired into `primitives/README.md`, the concept card, the individuation paper §4.7, and `Paper_054` §3.4. Census unchanged at 174.
131. **`θ_ind` vs the Q-COMPUTE WALL — INDEPENDENT, and the reason is that ‘individuation’ names TWO QUANTITIES. 2026-09-06.** **The answer to the question asked is NEGATIVE, and it was flagged as the likely outcome before starting.** `Paper_056`'s Class-A wall sits where `Γ_commit(M_cap) = Γ_individuation⁻¹`, and `Paper_054` §3.4 defines that timescale as *“the substrate's primitive timescale for **resolving multi-channel participation into single-channel participation**”* with `τ_individuation ~ τ_V1 = ℓ_ED/c`. **That is indexed by CHANNELS, is a RATE, and is scaled by the V1 kernel width. `θ_ind` is indexed by LOCI, is a dimensionless RATIO, and encodes a linear extent (#128).** **Different index set, different type, different scale: `θ_ind` appears nowhere in the wall's derivation and the wall's rate appears nowhere in `θ_ind`'s. No constraint in either direction, and none is manufactured.** **Recording the NON-connection is the point** — the identification is tempting (both called individuation, both living in `ℓ_ED`) and it is now closed off in writing, **with the factor of three that would have had to be explained written down beside it: `θ_ind = 1` is a 3‑`ℓ_ED` region, `τ_indiv` is one `ℓ_ED`.**

    **FINDING 1 — the term is overloaded across two arcs with ZERO cross-citation.** The individuation paper contains no reference to `Paper_054`/`Paper_056`, `Γ_individuation` or multiplicity; **no q-compute paper mentions `θ_ind`, `b_int` or `b_bdry`.** **Same class of defect as the ‘adjacency’ overload and the four-band senses — one word carrying several structures, found by following it across arcs.** **And the split is EXACTLY the one this session already had to name:** `P-Commitment-Advancement` (#117) was adopted because **P11 selects a CHANNEL and is silent on the LOCUS.** **The q-compute sense is the channel half; the individuation paper is the locus half. They are the two sides of the bridge that postulate had to build.**

    **FINDING 2 — the overload is INSIDE the source card, in ADJACENT sections.** `primitives/concepts/individuation.md` describes the **same substrate change** — boundary bandwidth to an external system going UP — twice, in consecutive subsections, in **opposite directions**: **§5.3** *“Post-measurement: chain is in one channel, **boundary-bandwidth is high, individuation is sharp**”* (individuation UP) versus **§5.4** *“slowly **loses internal-to-boundary bandwidth ratio. Its individuation weakens**”* (individuation DOWN). **By the promoted criterion `R = b_int/b_bdry`, `b_bdry` up means `R` DOWN, so BOTH should be down. §5.4 agrees; §5.3 does not — because §5.3 is using the channel sense**, in its own words *“chain spans multiple channels” → “chain is in one channel”*. **So the promoted paper adopted only §5.4's sense, and the MEASUREMENT/COLLAPSE reading of §5.3 is NOT covered by `θ_ind`** — which matters, because that reading is downstream-load-bearing. **Flagged in place rather than left as an apparent contradiction for someone to rediscover.**

    **FINDING 3 — §5.5's ‘best cut’ prescription is VACUOUS, and union closure is why.** The card says *“the best cut is the one with the **largest** `b_int/b_bdry` ratio”*. **But `R` is closed under union (#128 result B, 0 violations in 400 pairs) and `b_bdry(V) = 0`, so `R(V) = ∞`: maximising `R` selects the ENTIRE UNIVERSE, every time.** **The prescription picks out nothing.** **This is not a new problem — it is the union-closure result meeting a sentence written before it**, and the fix is not a different ratio: since the admissible family is upward-closed, **a cut must be chosen by something other than maximising `R`** — minimality, a stated system of interest, or the physics of the question. **The criterion CHECKS a cut; it does not FIND one.**

    **What is NOT claimed: that either arc's physics is wrong.** **Both are internally sound — the WORD is what is overloaded** — and nothing in the q-compute arc changes, nor does `θ_ind = 1`. **Nor is it claimed that the two senses should be unified; they may simply be two things.** Written back to the concept card (banner over §5.3–§5.5, §5.5 corrected) and the individuation paper (new §4.7). `foundations/Note_Individuation_TwoSenses_2026-09-06.md`. Census unchanged at 174.
130. **`ξ` TIGHTENED — the `± 0.3` was never an uncertainty, and `θ_ind = 1` goes from 0.78σ to 9.18σ. 2026-09-06.** **The thing worth fixing was not the seed count.** #129 left `θ_ind = 1` conditional on `ξ < 2` against a quoted `ξ = 1.8 ± 0.3`, a ~0.6σ boundary and a one-in-four risk. **Reading the estimator before running anything showed the ERROR BAR was the problem.** `ed_sc_3_1_xi_canonical.py` accumulates one `ξ` **per snapshot** into `all_xi_p` and reports `xi_std = np.std(all_xi_p)` — so the published `± 0.30` is the **per-snapshot SPREAD over 400 snapshots**, i.e. how much ONE SNAPSHOT varies, **not the uncertainty on the mean**. The same artefact's ten per-seed means have a spread of `0.16`. **Three different quantities were all being called “the error on `ξ`”, and only one of them answers ‘is the substrate's `ξ` below 2’.**

    **Measured, 40 seeds / 1600 snapshots, estimator IMPORTED from the canonical script so the method cannot drift: `ξ = 1.7397`, per-snapshot spread `0.3342`, per-seed spread `0.1793`, **standard error `0.0284`**.** **Distance to the bracket boundary `ξ = 2`: 0.78σ using the spread the corpus quoted, **9.18σ using the standard error**. So `θ_ind = 1` does not sit on a one-in-four risk — it sits on a 9σ bracket.**

    **AND THIS IS THE SECOND HALF OF #47's STORY, which makes it a two-sided correction.** The 2026-09-04 pass was **right** that `1.7575` over-states the precision — five significant figures on a number uncertain in its first decimal. **But its replacement `1.8 ± 0.3` substituted a spread for an uncertainty and UNDER-stated the precision by an order of magnitude.** **The corpus overstated the precision, then understated it, and both errors have ONE cause: spread and uncertainty were never distinguished. The supported figure is three significant figures — `ξ = 1.74 ± 0.03`.** **This also settles the rounding disagreement #126 flagged under C2** (#47's *“~1.8 ± 0.3”* vs soft-matter #2's *“1.76 ± 0.30”*): **both were quoting the spread**, and neither was wrong about the spread — only about calling it the error on `ξ`.

    **The honest caveat, stated rather than hidden behind the mean: 4 of 40 individual seeds exceed 2.0** (90% below). **`θ_ind` is a property of the RULE, not of one realization, so the mean is the right statistic — but a realization drawn from the upper tail would individuate at `θ_ind = 2`.** **CHECKED AND CLEAN:** the artefact's smoothed channel reproduces at **`3.017`** (vs `3.05`), a factor 1.74 away and **in a different `θ_ind` bracket** — **but that is not a corpus error.** `ed_sc_3_1_xi_canonical.py` labels it *“secondary, for audit”* and the raw density *“primary”*, and the papers quote the primary; **which is also the right channel for `θ_ind`, since raw participation content is what carries system–environment correlation.** **The choice is load-bearing and is currently correct rather than lucky** — worth recording as such. 129. **THE DECOHERENCE ROUTE WORKED — `θ_ind = 1`, conditional on `ξ < 2`; and it REFUTES the candidate I floated two hours earlier. 2026-09-06.** **The two accounts are the same QUANTITY, not just the same gap.** The individuation paper's §6 says it and `Paper_024_LindbladLimit`'s `P-Factorized-IC` *“should be read as two accounts of the same gap”*. **Stronger than that:** `P-Factorized-IC` asserts `ρ_tot(0) = ρ_S(0) ⊗ ρ_E(0)`, **no system–environment correlation**, and in ED that correlation is carried by cross-boundary shared channels — **V5 IS the cross-chain correlation kernel (`Paper_090`), and `b_bdry(S)` is exactly the shared-channel content across the cut.** So **exact factorization ⇺ `b_bdry(S) = 0` ⇺ `R(S) = ∞`: `P-Factorized-IC` IS INDIVIDUATION AT `θ_ind = ∞`.** **Which shows at once that it cannot fix a FINITE threshold by itself — as an exact postulate it names only the limit.** What fixes a finite value is its **domain of validity**.

    **And DEPTH IS QUANTIZED, which is what makes the answer sharp rather than approximate.** Correlations decay with `ξ`, so a locus at depth `d` from `S`'s boundary is still correlated with the outside when `d < ξ`. **If every locus of `S` lies within `ξ` of the boundary, `S` is all boundary layer, has no interior, and factorization has NOWHERE TO BE ACCURATE** — not *“is inaccurate”*, which is a different and weaker statement. **Depth is counted in loci, hence an integer**, so the condition is `⌈a/2⌉ > ξ`; with `R = (a−1)/2` from #128 the threshold is a **STEP FUNCTION of `ξ`**: `0<ξ≤1 → a≥2 → θ=0.5`; **`1<ξ≤2 → a≥3 → θ_ind = 1`** (measured `ξ ≈ 1.83`); `2<ξ≤3 → a≥5 → θ=2`. **So `θ_ind` does NOT inherit `ξ`'s uncertainty — only the question of which integer bracket `ξ` falls in.** **And a SECOND route reaches the same value using no `ξ` at all: the smallest square containing a locus not on its own boundary is `a = 3`, giving `R = 1`. Two independent routes, one value.**

    **THE SENSITIVITY, STATED PLAINLY, because it is the whole risk.** This needs **`ξ < 2`**. The corpus value is `ξ = 1.8 ± 0.3` (`Paper_096`, GR-SC 1.7 half-decay, 10 seeds), putting that boundary about **0.6σ away — roughly a ONE-IN-FOUR chance the bracket is wrong**, and `ξ > 2` would give `θ_ind = 2`. **So the result is sharp and FALSIFIABLE: tighten `ξ` and the answer is decided.** *(The probe's own 5-seed estimate is `1.831 ± 0.043`, but by a DIFFERENT estimator — 1/e crossing of the phase autocorrelation, not the GR-SC 1.7 half-decay — so it is NOT a tightening of the corpus value and is not quoted as one.)*

    **AND IT REFUTES MY OWN CANDIDATE FROM #128.** That note floated **`θ_ind = (ξ−1)/2 ≈ 0.4`**, flagged there as *“arithmetic plus an identification nobody has argued for”*. **Making the argument kills the number.** It identified `θ_ind`'s length with `ξ` directly, when **the boundary shell must be crossed on BOTH sides before an interior exists** — the required extent is `2ξ`, not `ξ`. **It also landed BELOW the minimal-pair value `1/2`, which would individuate every connected pair and make the criterion vacuous; that should have been the tell.** **The flag was right and the number was wrong — which is the flag doing its job, within the same day.**

    **Tier, and what is NOT claimed.** The identification (`P-Factorized-IC` = individuation at `∞`) is **argued**; the value is **`D-via-I` conditional on `P-Factorized-IC`'s domain AND on `ξ < 2`**. **It is NOT derived from the 13 primitives, and `F-IND-2` is STILL NOT FIRED** — unchanged from #128. **One measured observation against `Paper_024`:** the cross-boundary correlation fraction **never becomes small at any accessible region size** — at `R = 9.5` (a 20×20 region) about **11%** of near-range correlation mass still crosses. **The Born–Markov regime `Paper_024` postulates is a genuine IDEALISATION on this substrate, not an asymptotically-approached one** — consistent with the paper, which declares the regime rather than deriving it, but worth having measured. Written back to the individuation paper (§4.6, §4.5's refuted candidate corrected, audit row 7) and research target #25. `foundations/Note_ThetaInd_DecoherenceRoute_2026-09-06.md`. 128. **`θ_ind` CHARACTERIZED — it is a LENGTH, not a dimensionless constant, and one of the three named routes is CLOSED. 2026-09-06.** The individuation paper's **one undetermined quantity** (audit row 7, `I`), whose source concept asks twice: *“Structural constant? Regime-dependent? Tied to `ℏ` / bandwidth normalization?”* **No value is derived and none is claimed.** What is settled is what KIND of quantity it is — which decides what evidence could ever fix it. Four analytic results, each confirmed numerically (`event-density/theory/individuation_theta_probe.py`, new).

    **(A) THE `ℏ` / BANDWIDTH-NORMALIZATION ROUTE IS CLOSED.** `b_int` and `b_bdry` are both homogeneous of degree 1 in the edge weights, so `R` is invariant under `b → λb`. **Measured: `λ = 1000` moves `b_int` from `60.0` to `60000.0` and leaves `R = 2.500000` exactly.** **So no bandwidth normalization and no value of `ℏ` can supply `θ_ind` — there is nothing for a scale to attach to.** **One of the three named routes, eliminated. A banked negative.**

    **(B) INDIVIDUATION IS CLOSED UNDER UNION, FOR EVERY `θ`.** One line: `I_S > θB_S` and `I_T > θB_T` give `I_S+I_T > θ(B_S+B_T)`, and edges *between* `S` and `T` move from boundary to internal, which only raises the ratio. **Measured: 0 violations in 400 random pairs — disjoint, adjacent and overlapping.** **So NO choice of `θ_ind` makes this an object-COUNT criterion: two separate individuated systems always union to an individuated one. It is a cohesion filter, not a ‘this is one thing’ test.** **Downstream: §4's licence to the four-band classification is UNAFFECTED** (that licence is the sharpness of the edge partition, not object-counting) **but the bands' relativity to `S` is sharper — `S` is SELECTED, not found, because the admissible family is upward-closed.**

    **(C) `R` IS A LINEAR EXTENT, DIMENSION-INDEPENDENTLY.** For a side-`a` hypercube on a hypercubic lattice, in **any** dimension `d`: `b_int = d·a^{d−1}(a−1)`, `b_bdry = 2d·a^{d−1}`, so **`R = (a−1)/2` — the `d` and the `a^{d−1}` cancel.** Confirmed exactly for `d = 1, 2, 3`; **survives 60% bandwidth disorder** (−7.6% to +6.9% over five seeds) **and real committed morphology on the certified rule** (`R/(√|S|/2)` stays in `0.56–0.67` from `|S| = 4` to `200`). **So `R > θ_ind` reads ‘linear extent > `2θ_ind+1` loci’: `θ_ind` IS A LENGTH WEARING A DIMENSIONLESS COSTUME.** **Two boundary values: a single locus has `b_int = 0` hence `R = 0` — one thing alone is not individuated at all — and the minimal composite, two loci sharing an edge, gives `R = 1/2` EXACTLY, in every dimension.** *(Reading that as consilience with “two is more fundamental than one” is interpretive, not a derivation, and selects no value.)*

    **(D) — BUT ONLY WHERE THE SUBSTRATE HAS GEOMETRY.** On a geometric graph the cut scales like a surface and the interior like a volume; **on an EXPANDER the Cheeger constant is bounded below, the cut grows WITH the volume, and `R` stalls.** Measured, degree-4 lattice vs degree-4 random regular graph, `|S| = 4 → 256`: **lattice `R` runs `0.50 → 7.50`, expander `R` runs `0.30 → 0.56`.** **ED's metric locality is EMERGENT, not primitive — so `θ_ind`'s very CHARACTER changes across that transition: a length threshold in the geometric regime, a genuinely dimensionless cohesion threshold before it.**

    **What this does to `F-IND-2`, and the limit is deliberate.** The falsifier asks for *“regime-dependence that cannot be absorbed into bandwidth normalization”*. **(A) shows bandwidth normalization can absorb NOTHING — the escape route in its own wording is unavailable — and (C)/(D) show `θ_ind` is a length in one regime and not in the other.** **THIS IS NOT `F-IND-2` FIRED AND MUST NOT BE BANKED AS ONE.** It asks whether physics *needs* two thresholds; all that is shown is that a fixed `θ_ind` **is** a fixed length, so two regimes with different characteristic lengths would need different thresholds. **Whether ED has two such regimes load-bearingly is NOT shown.** `F-IND-2` moves from hypothetical to concrete and testable, **no further**.

    **What would now determine it: a length in `ℓ_ED`, not a bandwidth calibration and not `ℏ`.** Two routes. **(1)** The scale at which `Paper_024_LindbladLimit`'s postulated factorization (`P-Factorized-IC`) becomes accurate — the same gap from the other side, which the paper's §6 already says are *“two accounts of the same gap”*. **(2)** The measured substrate correlation length `ξ_canonical ≈ 1.8 ± 0.3 lu` (`Paper_096`), which by (C) gives **`θ_ind = (ξ−1)/2 ≈ 0.4 ± 0.15`**, just below the pair value `1/2`. **(2) is arithmetic from (C) PLUS an identification NOBODY HAS ARGUED FOR — a candidate to be argued or refuted, NOT a result.** **`θ_ind` remains undetermined; what changed is that it is no longer an unknown dimensionless constant, and the corpus has lengths.** Written back to `Paper_Individuation_TheSystemEnvironmentCut.md` §4.5 + audit row 7 + preamble, and to research target #25. `foundations/Note_ThetaInd_IsALength_2026-09-06.md`. 121. **`increment` REPLICATES THE SPLIT — and reveals that “the substrate fills” is also a regime statement. 2026-09-06.** The second undocumented parameter, tested exactly as `ρ*` was: each arm against the no-phase control at its **own** increment, `ρ*` held at `0.5`.

    **Ranking: identical at every increment** — `|acc|/n` > intensive `Coh` > `Grad` > canonical `Coh`, **the same order #120 found across `ρ*`.** **Binding: `|acc|/n` binds at `inc = 0.5` (`2.57` vs control `2.16`) and `1.0` (`3.77` vs `2.08`), and NOTHING binds at `2.0`**, where the control itself reaches `5.29`.

That is now a two-parameter result, and it is the session's central conclusion.**

    **NEW, and the sharpest thing in the run: the FILL result is also conditional, and it interacts with the phase term.** At `inc = 0.5`, of 2304 nodes: **control 2304 (full), `|acc|/n` 2285, intensive `Coh` 2243, `Grad` 1881 (82%), canonical `Coh` 1684 (73%).** **The extensive phase arms cause EXTINCTION — the front dies before filling.** At `inc = 1.0` and `2.0` everything fills.

    **And the mechanism was predicted algebraically before the run.** At `inc = 0.5`, `ρ` is reachable at `0, 0.5, 1.0, …`, so **the coherence target `ρ* = 0.5` becomes exactly attainable.** At `ρ_u = 0.5`: **staying scores `−(0)² − 0.5 − 0 = −0.50`; a fresh neighbour scores `−0.25 − 0 − 0.5 = −0.75`; an already-committed neighbour at `ρ = 0.5` scores `−0.50`.** **The rule becomes dwell-preferring AND ranks already-committed neighbours above fresh ones, so the front revisits instead of expanding — and dies.** **The certified rule forbids self-transitions (#116), so it cannot dwell: it takes the next-best move, a committed neighbour, and paints itself into a corner.**

    **What this adds. “The substrate fills” is a regime statement on two counts** — #119 found a `ρ*` ceiling (`ρ* = 2.0` → 4033/4096), and this adds an `increment` FLOOR **that interacts with the scoring**: the control fills at `inc = 0.5` while the extensive phase arms do not. **So it is a property of the parameters AND the scoring, not of the parameters alone.**

    **And the certified point sits in a narrow good region.** At `inc = 1.0, ρ* = 0.5`: everything fills, the best arm binds, nothing crystallizes. **Move `increment` DOWN and the extensive arms go extinct; UP and nothing binds; move `ρ*` UP and the substrate stops filling; DOWN and nothing binds.** **The certified parameters are surrounded by regimes where something fails — and nothing in the corpus says they were chosen for that, or for anything.**

120. **THE RANKING IS INVARIANT, THE BINDING THRESHOLD IS NOT — #111 splits into a general half and a conditional one. 2026-09-06.** #119's highest-value outstanding check, run: the `Coh`-vs-`Grad` comparison across `ρ*`, each arm measured against the **no-phase control at its own `ρ*`** (a control that does not move with `ρ*` would reproduce exactly the error #119 warns about).

    **The numbers**, 48×48, 3 seeds, `k_phase = 8`. **Control:** `5.68 / 2.08 / 1.19` at `ρ* = 0.0 / 0.5 / 1.0`. **`|acc|/n` (intensive `Grad`):** `4.63 / 3.77 / 1.55`. **Intensive `Coh`:** `1.97 / 1.63 / 1.06`. **`Grad` extensive:** `0.94 / 0.81 / 0.77`. **Canonical `Coh`:** `0.79 / 0.73 / 0.69`.

The 2×2's ORDERING is not an artifact of `ρ* = 0.5`.**

    **The half that is CONDITIONAL, and it is the absolute claim.** By the *binds* test — beating the no-phase control at the same `ρ*` — **`|acc|/n` binds at `ρ* = 0.5` (`3.77` vs `2.08`) and at `1.0` (`1.55` vs `1.19`), and NOTHING binds at `ρ* = 0.0`, where the control reaches `5.68` against the best arm's `4.63`.** **At `ρ* = 0` the no-phase control outperforms every phase-augmented arm.**

    **And the mechanism is the one #109 identified.** With `ρ* = 0`, `Coh = −ρ_v²` strongly prefers fresh low-density targets, so the front advances cleanly and revisits little; `ρ`-differences stay small, the transport connection `|A|` stays small, and reach is long. **Any phase bonus perturbs that clean front, and the connection cost exceeds the alignment gain.** Consistent with everything measured in #109–#110.

CONFIRMED and generalised:** #111's ranking — **`Grad` intensively scored is the best form at every `ρ*`, `Coh` the worst** — so the *which functional* question is answered independently of the parameter. **DEMOTED to conditional:** the claim that **the phase term supplies binding**. They do not lose their support; they gain a condition.** **Note the shape: #112 corrected WHICH TERM the paper measures; this corrects UNDER WHAT CONDITIONS the measurement holds. Neither retracts the paper; both narrow it.**

119. **`ρ*` IS UNDOCUMENTED AND IS A BIGGER LEVER ON PHASE REACH THAN ANYTHING THIS SESSION COMPARED — 2026-09-06. A calibration warning about my own numbers.**

    **The parameter has no justification anywhere.** `ρ*`, the *coherence target density* in the certified `Coh = −(ρ_v − ρ*)²`, is `0.5` in `SigmaCoeffs`. **A corpus-wide search finds no justification for that value, or for `increment = 1`, anywhere** — `ρ*` appears in `physics-papers/` only in this session's notes and an unrelated continuum memo, and `SigmaCoeffs` says only *“qualitative roles are fixed …; magnitudes are tunable”*. **But `ρ*` is not a magnitude, it is a TARGET**, and where it sits relative to the increment is qualitative: with `increment = 1`, `ρ` is reachable only at `0, 1, 2, …`, and **`ρ* = 0.5` maximises the minimum distance from the target to the reachable set — the most unreachable place it could sit.**

    **My prediction was WRONG and the measurement says so.** I predicted `Coh` would be **inert** at the certified `ρ*`, since `Coh(0) = Coh(1) = −0.25` and it cannot distinguish a fresh locus from a once-committed one. **Measured: `Coh` takes different values across candidates in 82% of decisions, and switching it off changes the winner in 40%.** **It is not inert — it decides two decisions in five.** The reasoning failed because candidates are not confined to `ρ ∈ {0,1}`: revisited loci reach higher densities where `Coh` differentiates sharply.

    **The result that matters.** Condition (C), 64×64, 5 seeds, sweeping `ρ*`: **`ρ* = 0.0 → ξ = 5.23`; `0.5` (certified) → `2.13`; `1.0` → `1.19`; `1.5` → `0.76`; `2.0` → `0.63`.** **`ξ` falls by a factor of EIGHT across the range, monotonically.** **And the scale is the point: this session's largest characterized effect was #110's `+81%` reach at matched connection (`ξ` `2.08 → 3.77`). Moving `ρ*` from `0.5` to `0.0` moves `ξ` from `2.13` to `5.23` — `+145%`, LARGER than the entire effect the session has been dissecting — and it is a parameter nothing justifies.**

    **What survives, and it is the important one. Knots-safety is ROBUST to `ρ*`:** `R` peaks at `0.121` (at `ρ* = 0`) against `R > 0.8` for a crystal verdict — **nothing crystallizes anywhere in the range**, so the result the July arc rests on does not depend on this parameter. **The fill result has a ceiling:** at `ρ* = 2.0` the substrate stops filling completely (`4033` of `4096`), so *“the substrate fills”* holds for `ρ* ≲ 1.5` and is not unconditional.

    **The calibration warning, and it applies to my own work today. Absolute `ξ` values are not properties of ED — they are properties of ED-at-`ρ*`-`0.5`.** **Every `ξ` quoted in #103–#118** — the `2.13` control, the `3.83` binding result, the `0.73` canonical-`Coh` failure — **is measured at one undocumented value of a parameter that dominates the observable.** **The comparisons remain internally valid**, since every arm ran at the same `ρ*`; **what is NOT established is that the ORDERING of arms is stable across `ρ*`.** **So #111's conclusion — `Grad` binds, `Coh` does not — is a result at `ρ* = 0.5`. It may well be general. Nothing here shows that it is.**

    **Owed:** **(1)** a justification for `ρ* = 0.5`, **or an explicit statement that it is a convention** and that absolute `ξ` values inherit it — the corpus currently says neither; **(2)** **re-run the `Coh`-vs-`Grad` comparison at two or three values of `ρ*`** — if the ordering holds, #111 is general; if it flips, #111 is a statement about one parameter setting. **This is the single highest-value check outstanding on this thread.** **(3)** the same question for `increment = 1`, equally undocumented, which sets the reachable lattice `ρ*` is compared against. `foundations/Note_RhoStar_2026-09-06.md`. Census unchanged at 174.
118. **A DWELL-CAPABLE RULE BREAKS NO CERTIFIED RESULT — and the PARAMETERS, not the rule, are what suppress dwell. 2026-09-06.** The question #117's adoption opened, answered for the result it can reach.

    **The change, minimal on purpose:** add `u` to its own candidate set, scored by the certified `Σ` exactly as any other candidate. Same functional, same commit chokepoint, nothing else touched.

    **The algebra predicts the answer before the run.** With `Coh = −(ρ_v − ρ*)²`, `Str = ρ_v`, `Grad = |ρ_v − ρ_u|` and `ρ* = 0.5`, **a self-transition has `Grad = 0`** — no gradient to fight — so `Σ(u→u) = −(ρ_u − 0.5)² − ρ_u` against `Σ(u→v_fresh) = −0.25 − ρ_u`. **At `ρ_u = 1` these are exactly equal (`−1.25`); at `ρ_u = 2` it is `−4.25` against `−2.25`; at `3`, `−9.25` against `−3.25`.** **With `ρ* = 0.5` and `increment = 1` a locus jumps from 0 straight PAST the coherence target, so dwell is never strictly favoured — only ever TIED, at `ρ_u = 1`, and disfavoured after.**

    **A second structural finding, forced by the run crashing.** **The certified tiebreak keys on `graph.bw(u, v)` — the EDGE bandwidth — and a self-transition has no edge, hence no key.** **So the certified rule assumes the winner is a neighbour in TWO independent places: the candidate set AND the tiebreak.** One modelling choice was unavoidable and is flagged rather than buried: the self-move gets the disorder **mean**, `bw = 1.0`, competing on the footing of an average edge rather than being handed a win or a loss by construction.

    **The result.** Condition (C), 64×64, 5 seeds. **Certified: `R = 0.027`, `ξ = 2.13`, dwell `0.0000`, `4096` nodes committed. Dwell-capable: `R = 0.017`, `ξ = 1.93`, dwell `0.0604`, `4096` committed.** **Dwell is reachable but marginal — 6% of decisions, all of them ties, exactly as the algebra predicts. The substrate still fills completely either way, so nothing goes extinct. And no certified result breaks:** `R` and `ξ` both fall slightly (`ξ` by `−0.20`, ~9%), **and both changes move AWAY from crystalline order — so Knots-safety is not merely robust under the dwell-capable rule, it is more comfortably satisfied.**

    **What it settles, scoped.** **Allowing dwell does not break Knots-safety and the substrate still fills.** **That is ONE certified result, tested — not “any certified result”**, and the claim is limited to what was run. **The more useful finding is WHY dwell is rare: not that the rule forbids it — it now permits it — but the PARAMETERS.** `ρ* = 0.5` with `increment = 1` overshoots the coherence target in a single commit, after which dwelling is strictly worse.

    **So adopting `P-Commitment-Advancement` LICENSES a dwell but does not PRODUCE one. A dwell-bearing substrate needs `ρ*` or the increment changed — a separate and much larger commitment than the postulate.** **That matters for the mass route:** the dwell arc wanted dwell for individual rest mass, and **the postulate was necessary and is not sufficient — the reference parameters suppress the phenomenon the arc needs**, and changing them is a change TO the certified substrate rather than an addition to it.

    **Limits, plainly.** **The dwell FRACTION is sensitive to the tiebreak choice** — `bw = 0` for the self-move and dwell never wins a tie, `bw = 2` and it always does. **The EXISTENCE of ties is robust (it is algebra); the `6%` is a consequence of assigning the disorder mean, and nothing in the corpus fixes it.** **Five seeds, one grid, one condition, one measured pair.** The holographic counts, the `ρ`-dynamics and the orientation stratification are **untested here.** `foundations/Note_DwellCapable_Test_2026-09-06.md`. Census unchanged at 174.
117. **`P-Commitment-Advancement` ADOPTED 2026-09-06 on AP's decision — census 173 → 174, two arcs unblocked, and the certified rule stops being an unstated assumption.**

    **The postulate, declared at `Paper_087` §P11 and entered in the registry:** *a commitment event that selects a **propagation-carrying** channel advances the chain's locus; one that selects a **non-propagating** channel does not.* **Propagation-carrying is not a new class** — Branch 3 (#93) makes the **Adjacency** class exactly the P05-transporting channels, so it reads: **commit into Adjacency and you move; commit into Internal and you stay.**

    **Why it was needed, from both ends (#116).** **P11 selects a CHANNEL and is silent on locus** — the four-tuple is `(C,K,u,t)` and commitment collapses to a single `K`, with nothing canonical saying that changes `u`. **The substrate evaluation selects a LOCUS** (`compute_candidates` → `admissible_neighbors`, `u` excluded from its own candidates; `update.py`: *“advance the front (active u → v)”*). **And the Higgs/dwell arc needs the converse — that a commitment may FAIL to advance.** **One missing sentence, two arcs, opposite ends.**

    **Tier: P, declared, `(prov)`.** **Not derivable from the canonical thirteen — which is precisely why it is named** — and provisional because derivation from P05 + P11 + the Branch 3 classification is not excluded. **A bridge between two primitives, not a new primitive.**

    **What it buys.** **(1)** The certified update rule stops being an unstated assumption: *“advance the front”* becomes **the special case in which every commitment selects an Adjacency-class channel.** **(2)** The dwell route is unblocked **at the sourcing level** — a commitment into the Internal class is now a licensed non-advancing commit. **(3)** `Paper_MassWithoutMass`'s *“ballistic-or-extinct … no dwell, hence no individual rest mass”* is clarified in place: **accurate about the reference rule, not a statement about ED.**

    **What it does NOT do, stated so the adoption is not over-read.** **It derives nothing** — not `σ_τ`, not the lepton masses, not any mass-sector result; **it removes a blocker, not a gap.** **And it changes no certified result**, because the certified rule remains a special case of it — everything measured on that rule stands. **Whether a dwell-capable rule would change those results is untested**, and testing it means changing the reference substrate, which is a much larger move than anything in this session. **The July retraction otherwise stands.**

    **And the adoption meets that retraction's demand on its own terms.** It asked that the dwell be *“proposed honestly as a **new candidate primitive refinement** … not asserted as something the existing 13 primitives already admit”* — **it is now a named, tiered, censused postulate rather than an assumption.** Its own objection is also answered: it argued there is *“no canonical internal band for a commitment to collapse into”*, and **Branch 3 supplied exactly that class**, eight weeks later from an unrelated direction.

    **Write-backs:** `Paper_087` §P11 + postulate registry; `Note_Dwell_SecondRepair_2026-09-05`; `Note_ChannelVsLocus_Selection_2026-09-06`; `Paper_MassWithoutMass_BindingInertia` glossary; `../event-density/theory/Higgs_Emergence/Dwell_Question_Answer.md`. **Census re-baselined: 174, 0 orphaned falsifiers.**
116. **The channel-vs-locus selection gap examined 2026-09-06 — “ballistic-or-extinct” is NOT forced by the primitives, it is the substitution; and it is the SAME gap the dwell arc is blocked on.**

    **The gap, verified in both sources.** **P11 selects a CHANNEL** — canonical `Paper_087`: *“a chain's **multi-channel participation collapses to single-channel participation**”*. **The certified rule selects a LOCUS** — `simulator/sigma.py`'s `compute_candidates(u)` returns `graph.admissible_neighbors(u)`, *“neighbors reachable via a NON-decoupled edge”*, **with `u` not in its own candidate set**; and `simulator/update.py`'s docstring: *“commit at the winner … → **advance the front (active u → v)**”*, lines 71–73 doing exactly that. **So the evaluation substitutes locus-selection for channel-selection and nothing states the bridge.**

    **The phenomenon is ALREADY corpus content — this discovers nothing.** `Paper_MassWithoutMass_BindingInertia`'s glossary: *“**Ballistic-or-extinct.** The certified rule: a front advances one hop at the maximal speed or dies; **no dwell**, hence no individual rest mass.”* Named, glossaried, consequence drawn; `PATH_A_STATE` records it as *“a fact about the code”*. **Sixth time this session that something I was about to call new was already recorded.**

    **What is NOT stated is the cause, and it changes the status. Ballistic-or-extinct is not a consequence of P11; it is a consequence of the substitution.** P11 collapses to **one channel**, and under **Branch 3 (#93)** the channels at a locus fall into classes of which **only the Adjacency class is P05-transporting** — the class carrying content *between* loci. **So a commitment selecting an INTERNAL-class channel is a commit that does not advance. That is a dwell, and P11 permits it.** **The certified rule cannot express it because its candidate set is neighbouring loci and excludes staying put. The restriction lives in the implementation, not in the primitive.**

    **And this reverses the July dwell retraction's own objection**, which argued that P11's silence on advancement *“does not help: there is no canonical internal band for a commitment to collapse into in the first place.”* **Branch 3 supplied exactly that class.** So the objection is answered — by a result eight weeks later from an unrelated direction.

    **What this changes, at the right strength. Ballistic-or-extinct becomes implementation-conditional rather than primitive-forced.** The glossary entry is accurate as written (*“the certified rule”*), **but anything reading it as a statement about ED rather than about the reference substrate is over-reading it.** In particular **“no dwell, hence no individual rest mass” is a property of the reference rule**, and whether any mass-sector conclusion depends on it being a fact about ED **is not checked here** — `Paper_MassWithoutMass` may argue mass-from-binding on independent grounds, and **no claim of damage is made.**

    **It is the same gap as the dwell arc's, from the opposite end.** #94 named the missing bridge as **`P-Commitment-Advancement`** — *a commitment selecting a propagation-carrying channel advances the chain's locus; one selecting a non-propagating channel does not.* **That is precisely this channel → locus bridge.** **So the substrate-evaluation arc and the Higgs/dwell arc are blocked on the SAME missing statement, approached from opposite directions** — one needs it to *justify* advancing, the other to *permit* not advancing. **And the certified rule assumes something STRONGER than the candidate postulate:** not “advances iff propagation-carrying” but **“always advances”** — **so the evaluation arc has been running on a stronger unstated identification than the one the dwell arc was told it would have to name and defend.**

    **Owed, and no defect is claimed.** Three things are now on record: **(1)** the bridge is unstated and the certified rule's version is stronger than the named candidate; **(2)** ballistic-or-extinct is implementation-conditional, and after Branch 3 the primitives admit a dwell; **(3)** **two arcs are blocked on one statement** — adopting `P-Commitment-Advancement` would unblock the dwell route *and* ground the evaluation rule, **but it is AP's call, it increments the census, and the certified rule would then be a special case of it.** **Honest limit: whether a dwell-capable rule changes any certified result is untested, and testing it means changing the reference substrate — a much larger move than anything in this session.** `foundations/Note_ChannelVsLocus_Selection_2026-09-06.md`. Census unchanged at 173.
115. **The normalization gap does NOT close — the two rules are over different index sets, the link is an ANALOGY, and #114 over-reached. 2026-09-06.** #114 found ED's commitment rule already normalized (`P-LinRate`: `Pr ∝ b_K*/Σ_K' b_K'`) and called it *“the strongest thing supporting #111”*, flagging the gap as owed. **Answered by reading, and the gap is structural.**

    **The two sets, from the sources.** **The probe's candidate set is LOCI** — `simulator/sigma.py`: *“Admissible next-states from u: non-decoupled neighbors”*, returning `graph.admissible_neighbors(u)`. **`P-LinRate`'s set is CHANNELS** — `Paper_003` §2 sums over `K'`. **And canonical P03 keeps them apart explicitly:** *“The substrate carries **discrete index sets**: a **channel index set** `$\mathcal{K}$` … and a **locus index set** indexed by substrate-graph positions `u`.”* **Two index sets, named separately in the primitive that supplies them.**

    **And they cannot be identified, for a reason that is load-bearing elsewhere.** The natural bridge is Branch 3's — the Adjacency class is the **P05-transporting** channels, and P05 transports **along edges**, so one might hope for a bijection with the admissible neighbours. **It fails:** `Paper_MS-I` §3 derives the gauge group **`SU(N)` from channel multiplicity**, `N` channels at a locus with the connection transporting all of them along each edge. **A bijection between channels and edges would force `N = 1` per edge and collapse `SU(N)` to `U(1)`.** **The candidate set's size is the admissible-neighbour count (≈ 4 on the probe's lattice); the channel set's size is `N`, the gauge multiplicity. Unrelated numbers — and identifying them would cost ED its gauge structure.**

    **So #114 over-reached and this corrects it.** **What survives: both rules are normalized.** In the one place the corpus specifies a commitment-selection rule, that rule is a **probability over its candidate set**, not a raw score — **so the simulator's argmax-over-raw-`Σ` remains an outlier against the corpus's only stated selection rule.** **What does not survive: that `P-LinRate` PRESCRIBES the probe's normalization.** It does not, because it normalizes over a different set. **The convention that makes `Grad` bind — dividing by the coordination number — has no derivation from `P-LinRate`, and calling it “corpus-consistent” overstated a structural analogy as a structural implication.** **Correct label: an ANALOGY between two normalized rules over different index sets. Suggestive, not derivational.**

    **What this does to support (7): nothing, and that is the point.** Support (7) is **empirical** — measured on the probe, `+81%` reach at matched connection, canonical `Coh` unable to match under any normalization tried. **It rests on the measurement and never rested on `P-LinRate`.** **What loses standing is #114's claim that the convention is structurally motivated:** it is currently **a scoring choice that works**, with a suggestive parallel and no derivation. **`Grad`'s tier (#113) is unaffected — six supports, one dynamical, still a proposal.**

    **The residue worth keeping, and it is well-posed for the first time.** **ED specifies a commitment-selection rule over CHANNELS (`P-LinRate`) and the substrate evaluation selects over LOCI** — and **nothing in the corpus states how a channel-level selection rule induces a locus-level one**, while every probe in the substrate-evaluation arc selects over loci. **Not a defect found here, and it may be routine — but it is the same class of gap as the `w(e)` scheme translation (#87) and the band-signature loss (#97): a rule stated at one level and applied at another with the bridge unwritten. Two of those turned out to matter.** `foundations/Note_NormalizationGap_2026-09-06.md`. Census unchanged at 173.
