    **The residue, named rather than assumed — and it is much smaller than “P04 has four bands”.** **`P-Band-Partition`:** *the four classes are disjoint and exhaustive over $\mathcal{K}(u)$.* **Four-ness is not derived** — P10 lists **three** rule-types (matter, gauge, kernel), a *different* trichotomy. **Exhaustiveness is not licensed.** **Disjointness is the clause that bites**, because a channel can both transport polarity (P05) and be phase-randomized at commitment (P11) — and #84 confirmed they **do** overlap on P05/P11 alone. **What separates them is individuation** (#85/#86): which side of `S`'s boundary the far endpoint sits on. **So `P-Band-Partition` is discharged only *relative to a choice of system `S`*, which makes the bands RELATIONAL, not intrinsic** — and that is a real change to how the bands are used, not a formality.

    **A second postulate named, and it fixes a claim P04 never supported.** The transport arc's audit row read *“Finite per-cell `b_adj^max` | **form-forced** | P04 finiteness + additivity”*. **Canonical P04 gives non-negativity, NOT an upper bound.** Now **`P-Locus-Bandwidth-Bound`** (*a locus's total `b(u)` is bounded above*), tier **P**. Compare `Paper_065_Monogamy`, which does this correctly — `P-V5-Budget` + `I-V5` + P04 additivity, each named. **The entanglement arc named its budget postulate; the transport arc had been assuming P04 supplied one.**

    **Census 171 → 173**, and the increment is the point rather than a cost: two things the corpus was assuming are now declared, tiered and attackable.

    **What each arc gets.** **Soft-matter transport survives as a RE-GROUNDING, exactly as its own 2026-09-04 flag predicted** — *“the shared-budget identification may well survive on P04 additivity plus P05-as-sole-transport without needing four *named* bands, which would be a re-grounding rather than a retraction.”* The Adjacency band becomes **the class of P05-transporting channels**, and **`F-COONSET` keeps its referent**. **Higgs / dwell is unblocked at the sourcing level** — July's retraction objected that the argument *“hung on P04 carrying a four-band partition … not in the canonical primitive”*, and under Branch 3 the partition is not in P04 and never needed to be — **but it still owes its second repair** (borrowing P12's `∇_adj` to interpret a P04 band), which Branch 3 does not touch.

    **And the circularity the card had to give up.** The card claimed the partition *“supplies the sesquilinear inner-product structure … (Paper 003)”*. **Branch 3 derives the bands from P05/P07/P10/P11, so it cannot then have them found the inner product those primitives are stated in — the two claims cannot both stand.** Independently, #91 showed `Paper_003` is **insensitive** to the partition anyway. Struck through on the card. **Files: `primitives/P04_bandwidth.md` (title + canonical statement amended, banner), `soft-matter/Paper_UnifiedP04TransportBudget` (Preamble 3, §2, two audit rows, banner), `Paper_087` §P04 (closure pointer — no change to the primitive's text).** `foundations/Note_FourBand_Branch3_2026-09-05.md`.
92. **`Paper_012_6_Heisenberg` checked 2026-09-05 (late) — it is a DIFFERENT four-band partition, the term is overloaded four ways, and the disputed partition has exactly ONE genuine paper-level dependent.** Path C item 3b, and the strongest input yet to AP's pending branch decision. **The reasoning for checking it was that an uncertainty relation plausibly *does* care how bandwidth is partitioned** — twelve mentions, the heaviest use in any paper. **It is not the same partition.** `Paper_012_6` §2.3 declares its own **`P-FourBand`** — **position (P03 adjacency) / momentum (P04 propagation) / time (P13) / energy (P-RB-1)**, the *conjugate pairs* — as a **named paper-local postulate already in the census, with its substrate derivation explicitly OPEN.** The disputed partition (`primitives/P04_bandwidth` §1.5) is **Internal / Adjacency / Environmental / Commitment-reserve**, partitioning `b_K` by **participation-partner type**. **Different partitions of the same primitive, sharing only a name.**

    **Four senses of “four-band” found** (`internal notes/_check_fourband_senses.py`, re-runnable): **(1)** the disputed partition; **(2)** `P-FourBand`'s conjugate pairs; **(3)** `Paper_004_5_Tsirelson_Discrete` §3.6, verbatim — *“The four-band partition (**Bell-test settings** `a, a', b, b'`)”*, **not a bandwidth partition at all** but four *correlation* bands from the experiment's design; **(4)** `Paper_003_5`'s *“adjacency-bandwidth”*, meaning bandwidth along graph adjacency (#91). **Eighteen of the mentions that looked like blast radius are senses 2 and 3. A grep for “four-band” overstates the disputed partition's footprint by roughly a factor of three.**

    **What survives:** **exactly one genuine paper-level dependent**, `soft-matter/Paper_UnifiedP04TransportBudget` (audit row *“P04 four-band partition; Adjacency = P05-transport content | P (primitive) | `primitives/P04` §1.5”*) — **and it already flags itself**: *“the load-bearing identification is a posit, not a theorem … the single most important thing to attack.”* `Paper_GR-I`'s three mentions are a **nominal** citation in a primitives list (its derivation uses reciprocal edge bandwidth `b_uv`, not the partition); `Paper_089` was **already corrected 2026-07-29** as archived-M-series and *“not load-bearing”*; `Paper_003` is insensitive (#91).

    **Two problems on the cards, both flagged in place.** **(a) The card tiers the partition more strongly than its own source concept does** — `primitives/P04_bandwidth` presents it as canonical primitive-level content while `concepts/participation_bandwidth` says *“The four-band structure is **motivated empirically**”* and lists *“whether it is mathematically forced”* as an open question, **twice**. Those are not the same claim, and the stronger one is the derived card rather than the source. **(b) The card claims a dependent it does not have:** *“The four-band partition (P04 §1.5) supplies the sesquilinear inner-product structure … (Paper 003).”* **#91 showed `Paper_003` needs channel orthogonality, not the partition.** Struck through with a correction banner rather than deleted.

    **What this does to the branch decision: it prices it, it does not decide it.** #82's conflict — the card asserting the partition in its own title while canonical `Paper_087` P04 does not mention it — is untouched. **But with one genuine dependent, which itself calls the dependency a declared posit, Branch 3** (bands as channel *classes*, disjointness licensed by individuation) **is far cheaper than the footprint implied** — as are Branches 1 and 2, for the same reason. **The honest residual: a low dependent count is an argument about cost, not about truth.** If the partition is right it belongs in `Paper_087`; if not, it does not belong in the card's title. **Still AP's call, now a cheap one.** `foundations/Note_FourBand_Senses_2026-09-05.md`.
91. **`Paper_003` checked against the four-band partition 2026-09-05 (late) — it does NOT need it, and the stronger finding is that it is INSENSITIVE to it.** Path C open item 3, and the input AP needs before deciding the four-band branch (#82/#83/#84). **Why it was open:** `Paper_003_BornRule` §2 lists among its upstream dependencies *“Paper #3 (Inner Product + Tsirelson): **four-band orthogonality** + sesquilinear inner product structure”* — the paper's one and only four-band mention, and the reason it was carried as a dependent.

    **The audit table answers it, because this paper has a good one.** §2.5 traces every load-bearing step and **no row cites the partition**: the participation measure comes from *Paper #1 + P04 + P09 + Paper #3's **inner product***; multi-channel participation from P02 + P07; frequency content from P11 + P04; the rate from **`P-LinRate`**, the paper's own labelled postulate; the frequency limit from `P-LinRate` + LLN; the identification from Paper #1. **The four-band phrase appears only in the dependency prose and never reaches the derivation.** Absence from a well-kept audit table is evidence, not a failure to find something.

    **And the structural reason is stronger than the absence.** **`Paper_003`'s only use of bandwidth is the normalized sum `Σ_{K'} b_{K'}`** — in `P-LinRate` and again in the frequency limit. **Any partition of the channel set leaves that sum unchanged.** Four bands, forty, or none. **So the Born rule is insensitive to the four-band question: it cannot need the partition, and — stated in both directions — it is not evidence FOR it either.**

    **What it does need:** P07 channel structure; **P04 additivity over the channel set**, which is a sum over **disjoint channels**, i.e. P04 exactly as canonically stated and not the over-a-region extension audited in #90; **channel orthogonality**, which is what *“four-band orthogonality”* was reaching for — the operative fact being that **distinct channels are orthogonal**, so `‖Ψ‖² = Σ_K b_K` carries no cross term (`Paper_007` §3.2), tracked as `P-Channel-Orthogonality` and **unrelated to how the channel set is partitioned**; and `P-LinRate`. **The #90 license clause double-covers the normalizer** — clause (a) by orthogonality, clause (b) because the Born rule is about **committed** outcomes and P11 commits in the channel basis only. Either alone suffices.

    **A naming collision found on the way, and flagged in place.** `Paper_003_5_ParticipationMeasure` §§3.3–3.4 use *“adjacency-bandwidth”* to mean **bandwidth along graph adjacency** (P03 locus indexing + P04: which edges a motif traverses, hence its accumulated phase). **That is not the four-band partition's “Adjacency band.” The words collide; the objects do not** — and a reader tracing the four-band question through this arc lands there and mis-reads it, which inflates the apparent footprint.

    **Consequence: `Paper_003` comes off the dependent list.** That shrinks the branch decision's blast radius without answering it — whether the partition is canonical is still #82's question and still AP's call. **Where the real dependents are**, by mention count, as a map rather than a verdict: `Paper_012_6_Heisenberg` **12**, `primitives/concepts/participation_bandwidth` 12, `PRIMITIVE_LOAD_BEARING_AUDIT` 7, `Paper_004_5_Tsirelson_Discrete` 6, `Paper_UnifiedP04TransportBudget` 6, `Paper_GR-I` 3, `P04_bandwidth` 3 — against `Paper_003`'s **1, and that one the prose line.** **`Paper_012_6_Heisenberg` is the one to check next:** the heaviest use in any paper, and unlike the Born rule an uncertainty relation plausibly *does* care how bandwidth is partitioned. `foundations/Note_Paper003_FourBand_Check_2026-09-05.md`.
90. **The DCGT check run 2026-09-05 (late) — NO live error, and the defect is corpus-wide rather than `Paper_073`'s.** Item 89 flagged `Paper_073` §3.2 for citing *“P04 (bandwidth additivity)”* with no regime condition and left the real question open: **does anything downstream inherit the clause where coherence survives the cell?** **105 papers cite DCGT**, so the question needed narrowing twice — first a proximity screen for coarse-graining near coherence content (55 papers), then to the operation actually at issue. **And the right search was not “who cites DCGT” but “who cites P04 additivity for an over-a-region sum”**, since canonical P04 states additivity **over disjoint sub-channels at a locus** and summing over *loci* is a different operation. That found eight sites. `internal notes/_check_dcgt_regime_users.py` (screen + adjudication, re-runnable).

    **The three coherence-critical papers do not inherit the clause at all**, which is the first useful finding: `Paper_004_6_Tsirelson_Continuum` coarse-grains **amplitudes** (`Σ_K → ∫`, `P_K → Ψ(x)`, sesquilinear in `Ψ`); `Paper_009_BerryPhase` coarse-grains a **connection**; `Paper_066_NoSignaling`'s L3 coarse-grains **operator support**. **None of them coarse-grains a bandwidth.** That is the right thing to do, and the corpus was already doing it without recording that it was making a choice.

    **And every site that does sum bandwidth over a region is licensed** — `Paper_065_Monogamy` sums over **partner chains**, which are disjoint channel sets, so it is P04 **as written** and not an extension; `Paper_067/068_VonNeumannEntropy` conditions explicitly on **independent** subsystems and already names it `P-Additivity` with an open item; `Paper_042_NoSingularity` sums **committed** content; `Paper_V5UnifiedBudget`'s `W_max = ∫F_V5 dμ` is the integral of a bounded finite-width kernel, where the additivity mention is decorative; `Paper_073` §3.2 is decoherent by its own §3.3.

    **The payoff is the common license, and it was statable in one clause.** Every site is fine for the same reason — **the summed contributions carry no relative phase** — and in ED that happens exactly three ways: **(a) disjoint channels** (P04 as written); **(b) committed content**, because **P11 commits in the channel basis only** — *“ED commits only in the channel basis … phase … is never a commitment basis”* (`Paper_QuantumLogicKeystone_GleasonReconstruction` §7) — **so committed content has no phase left to interfere and bandwidth *is* additive over a region for it**; **(c) a decoherent regime** (`Coh → 0`). **(b) is the interesting one: commitment is what makes bandwidth behave classically over a region** — the arrow doing structural work the corpus had not cashed out here.

    **And the error would not have run in the safe direction, which is why it was worth checking.** Phase-aligned contributions carry **more** total bandwidth than the additive sum (`n²` against `n`), so a bound derived from additivity would be **violated, not conservative** — by a factor up to the number of contributions. That is the exposure `Paper_042`'s no-singularity bound would have carried; **it is doubly safe because its bound is a named postulate (`P-Bandwidth-Boundedness`), not a consequence of additivity alone.**

    **Written back once, canonically, at `Paper_087` P04** — where the primitive being extended lives — rather than eight times; `Paper_073` §3.2 now points at it and names its clause, (c). **`Paper_073` turns out to be on the safe side of a rule it merely instantiates.** `foundations/Note_P04_AdditivityLicense_2026-09-05.md`; targets #25.
89. **— and the same-day check that revised it: (R) IS already a corpus commitment, DCGT uses the other merge rule, and `Paper_073` §3.2 carries an unstated regime condition.** Item 88 closed the fork but rested on requirement (R) — *a coarse-grained quantity is a property of the cell, not of the indexing inside it* — which I flagged as an assumption nothing in the corpus states. **Checked. It does state it.** `Paper_073` (DCGT) §3.2, *Well-definedness*: *“the average depends only on `(ū,t̄)` — the coarse-graining center — **not on the specific substrate channels enumerated within the cell**.”* **That is (R)**, and it is load-bearing: DCGT §1 says *“every continuum-level empirical prediction in the corpus traces structurally through DCGT.”* **The individuation result now rests on a demand the corpus already makes and cannot give up.**

    **But the check cut both ways, and item 88 needed correcting.** DCGT's very next sentence merges by **bandwidth** addition — *“By P04 (bandwidth additivity), substrate bandwidth contributions sum additively within the cell”* — not by amplitude. **Run (R) against that rule and the Cauchy argument forces the PRODUCT `c·b_u b_v`, a third candidate item 88 never considered.** Verified: under amplitude merging only the geometric mean satisfies (R); under bandwidth merging only the product does. **So the edge weight is REGIME-DEPENDENT and item 88's “uniquely” needed its regime attached.** What excluded the product in item 88 was the normalization `f(b,b)=b` — **my choice, and it was doing real work**, since the individuation *ratio* is a quotient of sums of `w` and the constant cancels. **`min` is excluded under BOTH merge rules**, so the half that mattered survives.

    **The two rules reconcile: they are one rule in two regimes.** `b_K(U) = Σ_a b_a + 2Σ_{a<b}√(b_a b_b)cos Δπ_{ab}` — **the cross term is exactly `Coh`.** Phase-aligned gives `n²` for `n` unit loci; phase-random gives `n` in expectation (Monte Carlo: `2.003, 4.012, 8.030, 16.078` for `n = 2,4,8,16`), **which is DCGT's bandwidth additivity. DCGT's rule is the `Coh → 0` limit of `P-Motif-Algebra`'s.**

    **Which regime individuation sits in, argued rather than assumed — because the worked examples do NOT decide it.** Every bandwidth in them is `0` or `1`, where the two weights coincide, and the product reproduces all six verdicts. So the argument comes from what individuation *is*: **the source concept's decisive example is a Cooper pair de-individuating on gaining a *condensate* channel, and its account of entanglement is that a pair is *one un-individuated complex*. Both are coherence statements, invisible at `Coh → 0`.** Individuation in the decoherent limit would not have the content the concept claims for it. **Revised verdict: `D-via-I` conditional on (R) + `P-Motif-Algebra` + the coherent regime** — (R) got stronger, the conditionality got one term longer.

    **Defect found in `Paper_073` on the way past, flagged in place.** §3.2 attributes bandwidth-additive merging to **“P04 (bandwidth additivity)” with no regime condition**. But **P04's stated additivity is over *disjoint sub-channels at a locus*; summing over *loci within a cell* is a different operation**, and canonical `Paper_087` §P12 says the general combination rule carries a cross term. **DCGT is entitled to the decoherent rule** — its own §3.3 averages out *“specific channel labels, specific commitment-event timings,”* which is the microstructure carrying the phase — **but the clause reads as a general consequence of P04, and it is not**, and the whole Wave-3 corpus inherits it. Dated note added at `Paper_073` §3.2. **Nothing in DCGT's own results changes.** Also: the item-88 note now carries a **read-§8b-before-citing-§6 banner**, because §§1–7 state the uniqueness unconditionally and would otherwise become the citable version. `internal notes/_check_merge_rule_regimes.py`; targets #25.
88. **The `w(e)` fork closed 2026-09-05 (late) — geometric mean vs `min` is not a preference, and the fork was the merge rule in disguise.** Item 87 wrote the map and recorded honestly that **`min(b_K(u), b_K(v))` satisfies all five stated requirements too**, so the geometric mean stood on the corpus's convention rather than on anything forced. **`min` was not a strawman** — the individuation ratio is a *cut* quantity and minimum-capacity is the canonical thing to put on a cut. **The obvious discriminator was inadmissible:** pointing at `Paper_030`'s bilocal `√(b_loc·b_horizon)` and at `a = a_N + √(a_N a_0)` restates justification #3 rather than testing it.

    **The construction uses a different established fact.** **`P-Motif-Algebra`** (`Paper_007` §2, a named postulate already in `Paper_087`'s census): *amplitude tuples form a complex vector space under componentwise addition* — so contributions combine by **adding amplitudes**, `b = |ΣP|²`, which is the rule canonical `Paper_087` §P12 already computes `Coh` on. Plus P03's *locus index set*, which is bookkeeping. **Requirement (R), regridding consistency:** merging two loci on the **same side** of a cut must not move `b_bdry`, since `b_bdry` is supposed to be a property of the cut.

    **Then the weight is unique, by Cauchy's functional equation.** With `w = f(b_u,b_v)` non-decreasing, (R) at phase alignment reads `f((x₁+x₂)²,b_v) = f(x₁²,b_v)+f(x₂²,b_v)`; setting `g(x)=f(x²,b_v)` gives `g(x₁+x₂)=g(x₁)+g(x₂)` with `g` monotone, so `g` is linear, symmetry forces `c(b_v)=c√b_v`, and `f(b,b)=b` fixes `c=1`. **`f = √(b_u b_v)`, uniquely.**

    **Four discriminating tests** (`internal notes/_check_edge_weight_discriminator.py`, re-runnable): merging `N` phase-aligned loci, **the geometric mean is exact for every `N` while `min` is wrong by a factor of `N`** — it saturates at the outside locus and stops seeing the system side; the misaligned-merge deficit is `b_K(v)`-independent under the geometric mean (**system-side regridding factorizes out of the environment side**, and the deficit is exactly the amplitude triangle inequality `Coh` already records) and `b_K(v)`-dependent under `min`; **regridding only the environment, `min` FLIPS the individuation verdict** (ratio `1.50 → 6.00` across `θ_ind = 2`) while the geometric mean does not move (`0.75 → 0.75`); and a scan of the power-mean family `M_p` from `min` (`p → −∞`) to the geometric mean (`p = 0`), **all correctly normalized so the family is a fair test**, gives regridding violations `8.91, 8.81, 8.59, 8.02, 6.69, 2.31` for `p = −8 … −0.1` and a **unique zero at `p = 0`**.

    **The result worth keeping: the edge weight was never an independent choice.** Had bandwidth itself been additive under merging rather than amplitude, the same argument gives the **product** `c·b_u b_v` and **both** candidates fail. **Whatever rule governs coarse-graining loci fixes the edge weight uniquely.** Note also what `min` fails *at*: it is regridding-consistent only where the minimum always falls on the same side, i.e. **exactly where it stops depending on both endpoints** and is no longer a shared-content measure. **Two things assumed and stated:** (R) itself — though the cost of rejecting it is that the individuation ratio is not measurable and `F-IND-1` is not a falsifier — and the reading of `P-Motif-Algebra` at **loci** rather than chains, which is the same operation on the same objects and is what `Coh` already does, but is an **extension of the postulate's stated domain** and is the one soft joint. **Verdict D-via-I conditional on (R) + `P-Motif-Algebra`, not unconditional and not `D`.** No postulate named; census unchanged. `foundations/Note_EdgeWeight_Discriminator_2026-09-05.md`; paper §2.1 + audit row 4.
87. **The `w(e)` map written 2026-09-05 (late) — and the gap turns out to be a scheme translation, not an oversight.** **Diagnosis first, because it is better than “unstated”: the two schemes attach bandwidth to different objects.** The earlier primitive set — now `primitives/concepts/` — put it on **edges**: *“**Primitive 04 supplies edge weights** — a positive real number on each edge measuring bandwidth … `w: E → ℝ≥₀`”* (`participation_bandwidth.md`), with `event_density.md` writing `G = (V, E, w)` and *“edge weights `w = b`”*. **Canonical P04 puts it on (channel, locus): `b_K(u,t)`.** **Individuation was written in the earlier scheme and never translated** — the same fossil AP identified in the “Primitive 10” header, and the third artefact of that scheme change found tonight.

    **The map: an edge carries what its endpoints share.** **`w(u,v) = Σ over K ∈ $\mathcal{K}$`(u) ∩ $\mathcal{K}$`(v) of √(b_K(u) b_K(v))`**, summing only over channels participated at **both** endpoints (P02's four-tuple supplies membership). **Five reasons:** non-negative as P04 requires and the ratio needs; **it is the amplitude product** `|P_K(u)|·|P_K(v)|`; **it is the corpus's existing convention for two-locus shared content** — `P-Quadratic-Strain`'s cross term and `Paper_030`'s bilocal `√(b_loc·b_horizon)` are both this geometric mean; it vanishes on unshared channels; it is symmetric.

    **Verified against the source concept's own three worked examples** (`internal notes/_check_edge_weight_map.py`, re-runnable): shielding raises the ratio and leakage lowers it; **an entangled member is not individuated alone (`0.00`) while the pair is jointly (`∞`)**; and **the same Cooper pair individuates in a normal metal (`∞`) and de-individuates in a superconductor (`1.00`) purely from gaining a shared condensate channel** — the concept's own illustration that individuation is relational. **The check discriminates:** a map summing all channels at both endpoints rather than only shared ones makes the metal and superconductor cases identical.

    **A unification falls out.** The phase-sensitive sibling of `w(e)` is `Coh`: on an edge the Dirichlet cross-term is `2Σ_K √(b_K(u)b_K(v))cos Δπ_K`, so **`|Coh_(u,v)| ≤ 2 w(u,v)`, with equality at full phase alignment.** **`w(e)` is `Coh`'s envelope with the phase stripped** — individuation asks how much is shared, `Coh` asks how much of it is in phase. **So the edge weight the system/environment cut needs and the coherence term settled this morning are one object read two ways.**

    **Honest alternative recorded rather than buried:** `min(b_K(u), b_K(v))` satisfies all five requirements too — a *bottleneck* reading against an *amplitude-product* one. **The geometric mean is chosen because it is the corpus's existing convention and the amplitude product, not because it is forced**, and a construction distinguishing them would be a real test. **Audit row 4 moves OPEN → D-via-I; `θ_ind` (row 7) is now the paper's only undetermined quantity.** `foundations/Paper_Individuation_TheSystemEnvironmentCut.md` §2.1.
86. **Individuation PROMOTED 2026-09-05 (late) — concept → tiered construction, verdict M3.** `foundations/Paper_Individuation_TheSystemEnvironmentCut.md` (new). **Promoted on the finding that three arcs independently depend on it** (#85): the `Str(C)` chain-level aggregation, the four-band classification, and the layer seam. **Not made a fourteenth primitive** — the corpus is committed to thirteen, and the construction is shown derivable from **P02 + P03 + P04**, with P07 upstream and P11 downstream. **Provenance corrected rather than flagged as an error, on AP's account:** the `primitives/concepts/` files *were* an earlier primitive set, which is why individuation carried a “Primitive 10” header colliding with canonical P10 (rule-type). **That is history, not a competing claim**, and the paper says so.

    **Tiering.** `b_int(S)/b_bdry(S) > θ_ind`, with the **form derived** and **`θ_ind` inherited and undetermined** — the source concept flags it open twice. **Verdict M3.** Nine-row audit table, **no `A` rows**, with the two honest weak points named: step 7 (`θ_ind`) and **step 4, a genuinely unstated bridge — `w(e)` is an *edge* weight while P04 gives `b_K(u)`, a *channel-at-locus* weight, and the corpus never states the map.** The natural reading (edge weight aggregates channels participated at both endpoints) is plausible and written nowhere. **Same class of gap as the `Str(C)` bridge, and recorded as the paper's first open item.**

    **The distinction the paper turns on, because conflating the two is the trap: given `S`, the edge partition is EXACT** — every edge has both endpoints in `S`, exactly one, or none, with no third case and no overlap — **while whether `S` counts as a system is GRADED.** So **disjointness is free once `S` is fixed, and what is not free is fixing `S`.** That is the whole content of the cut, and it is what licenses the four-band classification's disjointness that #84 showed P05/P11 cannot supply.

    **The cost is carried, not buried: band membership becomes relative to `S`.** `primitives/P04_bandwidth.md` states *“four mutually orthogonal substrate-level bands”* as absolute and `Paper_UnifiedP04TransportBudget` §2 partitions *“each locus's finite bandwidth `b(u)`”* with no `S` in it. **Under this paper there is no “the Adjacency band at a locus”, only “… with respect to `S`”** — not fatal if the transport arc's `S` is its own cell, but that identification is unstated and should be written.

    **Three named falsifiers, and they are in the master list as ML 4.15** so the predictions cross-check does not orphan them (checklist item 23). **`F-IND-1` is the sharp one:** an operationally sharp system/environment split whose boundary-bandwidth ratio is *not* extreme refutes the identification outright.

    **Position stated honestly against the standard situation: differently, not obviously better.** Standard treatments postulate a tensor factorisation, and the corpus already does this explicitly in `qft/Paper_024_LindbladLimit`'s `P-Factorized-IC`. **This paper replaces a postulated factorisation with a constructed ratio carrying a free threshold** — a gain in *kind* (the cut becomes a substrate quantity with measurable signatures) and **not yet a gain in determinacy**, since `θ_ind` is undetermined and the criterion is graded. **The two should be read as two accounts of one gap, not one superseding the other.** **No new postulate named; census unaffected.**
85. **The system/environment cut EXISTS 2026-09-05 (late) — it is individuation, it answers all three of tonight's convergent questions, and it makes the bands *relational*.** **#84 said the cut was absent. It is not — the fourth time this session I would have claimed absence wrongly, and like the other three it was caught by opening the folder.** `primitives/concepts/individuation.md` gives it operationally: `b_int(S) = Σ_{both ends in S} w(e)`, `b_bdry(S) = Σ_{one end in S} w(e)`, **S individuated iff `b_int/b_bdry > θ_ind`** — self-described as *“the ED account of **where one thing ends and another begins**.”* **And it does the work: every edge has both endpoints in `S` or exactly one, a sharp exhaustive partition given `S`. So the band classes ARE disjoint, with the cut supplied by individuation rather than by P05/P11 alone.** **It answers all three questions, and they were one question.** **`Str(C)`'s** *whose channels?* → those of the individuated complex `S` containing `C`. **The bands'** *how are Adjacency and Environmental disjoint if both use P05?* → by which side of `S`'s boundary the far endpoint sits. **The layer seam's** *what crosses?* → boundary bandwidth — individuation's own thick-regime reading is *“a rapid drop in `b` across a boundary”* with *“decoherence … the slow dissolution of this drop.”* **Three arcs, independently, in one night, and one 2026-04-24 concept document answers all three — which none of them cites.** **Three things travel with it.** **(1) It is not one of the 13.** The file is headed *“Primitive 10 — Individuation”* while canonical `Paper_087` §P10 is **rule-type**, and `primitives/P10_rule_type.md` sits in the same tree carrying it — **a stale header from an older numbering, now flagged in place.** Its own status line reads *“First-pass canonical draft.”* **Anything grounded on it is grounded on a concept, and that must be said** — exactly the sourcing discipline this two-day question has been about. **(2) `θ_ind` is undetermined and the document says so twice:** *“Structural constant? Regime-dependent? Tied to `ħ` / bandwidth normalization?”* **The cut exists; its location is a free parameter.** **(3) The bands become relational, and the card says otherwise.** Individuation is explicitly *“relational, not intrinsic … change the environment, change the individuation status”* (its example: a Cooper pair is individuated in a normal metal, un-individuated in a superconductor). **So band membership is relative to a choice of `S`** — while `primitives/P04_bandwidth.md` states *“four mutually orthogonal substrate-level bands”* as absolute and `Paper_UnifiedP04TransportBudget` §2 reads P04 as partitioning *“each locus's finite bandwidth `b(u)`”* **with no `S` in it. 84. **Band-overlap check run 2026-09-05 (late) — they DO overlap, #83's free additivity fails, and what must be postulated is a system/environment cut ED does not have.** **Sourced in three steps.** **(1) Decoherence in ED *is* V5-mediated:** `Paper_QuantumDarwinism_RecordBandwidth` §3 table — *“Imprinting (decoherence) | **V5-mediated cross-chain correlation formation**, retarded, finite-memory `τ_V5`”*, citing `Paper_090` §3. **(2) V5 requires P05:** `Paper_090`'s closing statement — *“V5's identity is **conditional** on the postulated primitives P02 + P04 + **P05** + P07 + P09 + P10 + P11 + V1 inheritance.”* **(3) Therefore every Environmental (decohering) channel is a P05-transporting channel** — and under the card's own wording (*“content shared with neighbouring loci **via P05 polarity-transport**”*) **Environmental is not disjoint from Adjacency; it is arguably contained in it. So P04 additivity does not give the band totals — the sums double-count.** **The repair exists and its cost is exact.** The two can be separated by **counterparty** rather than mechanism — Adjacency shares with *neighbouring loci in the chain's own structure*, Environmental with *the bath* — which is almost certainly what the card intends. **But that requires ‘the environment’ to be a substrate-level distinguishable thing, and no primitive defines one.** Canonical P11's operational content *uses* the word (*“discrete, **environmentally**-phase-randomizing”*) while its statement proper says only *“the un-selected channels' phase content randomized”*; **P01–P13 give loci, channels, chains, rule-types and commitment, and none of them partitions ‘my structure’ from ‘the bath.’** **So #83 understated the residue.** Disjointness is **not** an independent assumption one can simply name — **it presupposes a substrate-level system/environment cut, which is the factorization / Heisenberg-cut problem in its usual form.** Revised residue: **(a)** a criterion distinguishing *own neighbour* from *environment*; **(b)** given (a), disjointness and exhaustiveness; **(c)** a per-locus bound on `b(u)`, which canonical P04 does not supply. **(a) is new and it is the expensive one.** **Third arrival at the same question tonight, and that is the real signal.** The `Str(C)` bridge ended on *whose channels does the radial sum run over*; the layer seam turns on what crosses when a layer-1 functional is read from layer 2; and the band classification turns on separating a chain's own neighbours from its environment. **All three are: where does the chain end and the rest begin? Three arcs, independently, in one night, and nothing in the corpus treats it as one object.** **What survives.** **Adjacency (P05), Commitment-reserve (P11) and Internal (the remainder) are disjoint by construction and additive with no new postulate** — a **three-class** reading works free. **But Environmental is load-bearing elsewhere**: `primitives/concepts/participation_bandwidth.md` uses it for double-slit visibility, qubit `T2`, and *“coherence engineering in quantum hardware is ED-bandwidth engineering”*, so dropping it is not free for the QM and q-compute arcs. **Soft-matter survives either way**, since Adjacency is clean under both readings. **Status of Branch 3: not dead, still better than Branches 1 and 2** (no amendment to `Paper_087`, no archived source) **but its headline was wrong** — the bands are classified, not derived-with-additivity-free. **Revised recommendation: adopt the three-class version now and treat Environmental as an open item rather than a band**, which re-grounds soft-matter immediately, leaves `Paper_087` untouched, and parks the QM/q-compute usage alongside the two other arcs waiting on the same system/environment criterion. `foundations/Note_BandOverlap_Check_2026-09-05.md`.
83. **Branch 3 worked 2026-09-05 (late) — the bands are channel *classes*, not a decomposition of a scalar. The conflict dissolves and the postulate shrinks.** **The card locates them in the wrong primitive.** They are not a decomposition of one channel's `b_K`; they are a **classification of the channel index set $\mathcal{K}$`(u)`**, and **P04's additivity — stated over *disjoint sub-channels* — then gives the band totals free**: `b_band(u) = Σ_{K ∈ band} b_K(u)`. **Every band has a licensing primitive and none of them is P04.** **P07** makes channels *“structurally distinguishable carriers with intrinsic identities”*, so a classification of $\mathcal{K}$ is a substrate fact rather than bookkeeping. **P10** makes rule-type *“a primitive label”*, so classifying by it **reads** a label rather than adding one. **P05** gives Adjacency; **P11** gives Commitment-reserve and, via *“un-selected channels' phase content randomized”*, Environmental; the remainder is Internal. **So both documents come out mostly right, and the two-day flip was about location, not existence:** `Paper_087` §P04 is correct that P04 is the scalar plus additivity; the card is correct that a four-band structure exists; **the card's error is one preposition — the bands are not *in* P04, they are *summed by* it.** **No amendment to `Paper_087`, no appeal to the archived paper.** **What Branch 3 does NOT deliver, named precisely, because that is the point:** **(1) four-ness** — P10 says *“multiple”* and lists **three** (matter, gauge, kernel), a *different* trichotomy; **(2) exhaustiveness**; **(3) disjointness, which bites** — a channel can both transport polarity (P05) and be phase-randomized at commitment (P11), and **if the classes overlap, P04 additivity no longer gives the band totals; the sums double-count**; **(4) a per-locus bound** — **canonical P04 gives non-negativity, not an upper bound**, yet `Paper_UnifiedP04TransportBudget` §2 says *“a fixed fraction of the **finite** `b(u)`”*. **Compare `Paper_065_Monogamy`, which does it correctly:** its finite budget comes from **`P-V5-Budget` + `I-V5`** (V5's finite total weight, inherited from `Paper_090`) **+ P04 additivity**, each named. **The entanglement arc names its budget postulate; the transport arc assumes P04 supplies one.** **So the honest postulate is “the classes are disjoint and exhaustive over $\mathcal{K}$`(u)`, and `b(u)` is bounded” — nameable, tierable, and far weaker than granting P04 a partition it does not have.** **One circularity the card must give up:** it also claims the partition *“supplies the sesquilinear inner-product structure”* (Paper 003). **Branch 3 derives the bands from P05/P07/P10/P11 and so cannot have them found the structure those primitives are stated in.** Either derived or foundational, not both — and whether `Paper_003` needs the partition at all, or only P07 distinguishability plus P04 additivity, is unchecked and is the cheapest next look. **What each arc gets.** **Soft-matter survives, exactly as its own flag predicted** (*“may well survive on P04 additivity plus P05-as-sole-transport … a re-grounding rather than a retraction”*) — **Branch 3 is that re-grounding**, with Adjacency becoming *the class of P05-transporting channels at a locus*; 81. **Koide read through `Str`/`Coh` 2026-09-05 (late) — an exact re-expression, no derivation, and the external reading's central premise is one ED already tested and rejected.** **The observation, and it is exact:** with `P_i = √m_i`, Koide's denominator `(Σ√m_i)² = |ΣP_i|²` is **`Str + Coh`** with every `cos Θ_ij = 1`, so **`Q = Str/(Str + Coh)`** and **`Q = 2/3 ⇺ Coh = ½ Str ⇺ Σ_C = −½ Str`.** Checked on PDG values: `Coh/Str = 0.500014` against `0.5`; `Q = 0.66666051`. **That is Koide's known precision restated in P12's decomposition — the same diagonal/off-diagonal split settled in #71.** **What it is not: a derivation.** Nothing in ED predicts `Coh = ½ Str` for the charged leptons, and writing a known relation in substrate notation is not evidence for the substrate. **`Paper_029`'s own history is the caution** — a relation restated in ED language read as a result for months until the prior art was checked (#60). **And the corpus already ruled on this class:** `arcs/arc-M/mass_ratio_constraints.md` §6.3, on `main`, says *“Koide-relation-style formulae … **not derivable from ED primitives at this stage**”* and *“generation-count predictions (3 generations): **not derivable**”*, with a principled reason rather than a shrug: *“ED's primitive structure produces classifications and dichotomies cleanly, but **does not produce continuous numerical relationships**.”* **That verdict stands untouched.** **The one new thing, tiered low on purpose:** Koide sets every `cos Θ_ij = 1`, which in ED's decomposition is a *special* configuration rather than a convention — so the ED reading of Koide's exactness is *the three amplitudes are fully phase-aligned*. **Weight: low.** Koide has no phases in it; the alignment is an artifact of `√m_i` being real and positive by construction, **not a physical finding.** **And where the external reading conflicts with the corpus, which is the useful part:** it rests on **mass = commitment-bandwidth consumption = the particle's clock rate**. **`Paper_MassWithoutMass_BindingInertia` §5 measured that and separated them** — a commitment-memory front dwells but its forward drift tracks its path speed exactly (`v_x/path = 1.00` at every memory level, **no directional inertia**), while a *bound* composite drifts at `0.72` against an unbound cluster's `0.97`. Boxed conclusion: *“the commitment rate and mass are different phenomena … the commitment/sparseness rate governs the clock rate, which is **time dilation** … while **mass is a separate thing that comes from binding**.”* **So the external reading is a faithful development of the premise it was handed; the premise is what ED contradicts.** `foundations/Note_Koide_In_StrCoh_2026-09-05.md`.
80. **`Str(C)` bridge written 2026-09-05 (late) — it is two statements, only the second carries content, and an unnamed load-bearing postulate sits behind it.** **Written, and the useful result is that the three audits that flagged it were not flagging the same thing.** Arm B's `D07a` and the blind run's GPT gap 1 both asked what the aggregation **is**; the blind run's Claude gap 4 asked what it **runs over** (*“a stated measure `μ(R′)`”*). **Reading all three as one item is why it looked like a single missing sentence with wide reach. It is one trivial sentence and one substantive postulate.** **The bridge itself costs nothing.** `Coh(C)`, `Str(C)`, `Grad(C)` are plain sums of their per-channel counterparts over $\mathcal{K}$`(C,u,t)`, the channels `C` participates in at `(u,t)` — P02's four-tuple, restricted. **The notation was already in the corpus:** `Paper_001_PreIndividuation` §3.1 writes `b_K^C(u,t)`, `π_K^C(u,t)`, `K ∈ $\mathcal{K}$`. **And the sum is plain rather than weighted, by P04 additivity** — a quantity built from `b_K` and aggregated non-additively would contradict the primitive it is built from, **so GPT's alternatives (weighted sums, maxima, graph integrals) are excluded rather than merely unchosen.** Written into `Paper_087` §P12. **All the content is in the second statement, and nobody had flagged it.** What $\mathcal{K}$ *contains* for a chain at radius `R` is a postulate, currently split across two clauses of `Paper_030`: **`P-Bilocal-Count`** (§4.2 — **named**, tiered, with falsifier `F-BC`) and **the radial `1/R` channel density** (§4.3 — **unnamed, un-tiered, one clause of justification**). **The unnamed one is what turns an `R`-independent per-channel strain into a logarithm, and therefore what produces `√(a_N a₀)` rather than nothing.** Flagged in place. **And it relocates the locality question rather than dissolving it.** A channel in $\mathcal{K}$`(C,u,t)` is one `C` participates in **at its own locus**; a *bilocal* channel by construction also carries content from a second source region — **so the far ends are not foreign loci `C` reaches into, they are the second sources feeding channels `C` already touches.** Coherent. **The price:** the radial density becomes a claim about **how many distinct channels one chain participates in at one locus, as a function of its distance from a mass**, and *“substrate-graph radial-projection geometry”* is a gesture at a derivation rather than one. **Three process notes, all against me, and two are serious.** **(1) A destructive bug in my edit helper zeroed `Paper_087_13Primitives.md`** — it called `open(p,"w")`, which truncates, and *then* raised on encoding. Restored from git intact (49,009 bytes, verified). **(2) I then reproduced the same bug and zeroed this ledger — and committed and pushed the truncation** (215 lines) before catching it. Restored from `HEAD~1`, verified at 299,176 bytes with all 79 prior items present. **Both files were committed, so nothing was lost; neither would have survived if they had not been.** Helper rewritten to encode first, write to a temp file, `os.replace` atomically, and **refuse any result less than half the original length**. **(3) The census caught a third error on the same turn:** I wrote that naming the new postulate in a paper was AP's call, then wrote the name into `Paper_030` — count went **171 → 172**, the script flagged it, and the name now appears in the note only. **All three instruments fired on my mistakes tonight, not the corpus's.**
79. **Aggregation rule investigated 2026-09-05 (late) — a sign error found in `Paper_030` §§4.3/5.2 that makes MOND repulsive, and the aggregation itself downgraded from missing structure to the known chain-level bridge.** **Started on the aggregation rule and found a sign error in `Paper_030` on the way.** §§4.3 and 5.2 publish `Σ_cross(R) = −√(GMa₀)\log(R/R₀)`. **With that minus, `a_r = −dΣ_cross/dR = +√(GMa₀)/R` — outward. MOND comes out REPULSIVE.** Verified at `R = 5×10¹⁹ m`: `+2.53×10⁻¹⁵` against a target of `−2.53×10⁻¹⁵`. `internal notes/_check_030_cross_sign.py`, re-runnable. **`Paper_QuadraticStrain_v1` §5.1 carries the same quantity with a PLUS**, and that one is right — independently confirmed by #71's three-way check, which places the cross term in `+Coh`. **So two gravity papers disagreed on this sign and `Paper_030` had it wrong.** Corrected in §§4.3 and 5.2 with a banner rather than silently. **How it survived: §5.3 writes** `a = −dΣ_N/dR − dΣ_cross/dR = GM/R² + √(GMa₀)/R`, **quoting the Newtonian term as a magnitude** (its signed value is `−GM/R²`) **and the cross term at face value, then adding them as though both pointed inward.** **The stated result of §5.3 is correct; the `Σ` it is derived from is not.** **No downstream result moves** — `a = a_N + √(a_N a₀)` was always right. **And the same error appeared twice today in an external family.** GPT reverse-engineered `Σ_H = −k\ln R` (#77 §7) and then answered *“B and C both work”* by dropping the same sign (#78). **An outside reader independently reproduced the corpus's own published error, twice, without having seen it.** That is the strongest evidence yet that this particular sign is a genuine trap rather than a slip — and it retroactively raises the value of #71, which was built to settle exactly it. **Now the aggregation question itself, and my framing of it last night was wrong.** Two external readers objected that the per-channel cross term is **exactly R-independent**, so a pointwise P12 gives it zero gradient, and one classified the radial integral as **(ii) missing structure** — *“an entire mathematical object (a path-aggregation rule with a specific measure) that P12 as stated doesn't contain.”* **They were reading it as a path/history integral. It is not one.** `ρ_bilocal(R')` is a **channel density**, so `∫ρ dR'` is a **sum over the chain's channels, which happen to be distributed radially** — and **P12 already requires exactly such a sum**, since it defines chain-level `Str(C)` while `P-Quadratic-Strain` defines per-channel `Str_K`. **So the aggregation is not a missing structure; it is the already-known chain-level bridge** — arm B's `D07a`, and gap 1 of the blind Σ_C run, both of which name the `Str(C) = Σ_K Str_K` step as unstated. **Last night's recommendation over-classified it**, and #77 §4's scope correction should be read with this: the log **is** licensed once the channel sum is granted; what is not stated is the sum itself. **One residual, narrower and worth keeping:** the integral runs `R₀ → R`, so it sums channels anchored at **other loci**. Whether those are the chain's own channels or ambient structure is not said, **and that is the same locality question the layer seam raises.** **Net for the night: one real defect fixed, one open item downgraded.** The defect is the kind a referee finds in the first pass — a published potential whose gradient points the wrong way. The downgrade means the gravity arc's aggregation is on firmer ground than #77 recorded, at the cost of routing through a bridge that three separate audits have now flagged as unstated. **`Str(C) = Σ_K Str_K` is named by arm B, by the blind run, and now by this — and it is one sentence.**
78. **S2 retest run 2026-09-05 — 2/3 reach C when handed the candidates; S2 closes; and the same family drops the same sign for the second time.** **Up from 0/3 blind.** Gemini and Claude both reach **C** with correct working. **GPT answers “B and C both work”, which is wrong**: it stated `X'(R) = +√(GMa₀)/R` correctly and then wrote `dΣ_B/dR = −dD/dR − dX/dR = GM/R² + √(GMa₀)/R`, **which requires `−dX/dR = +√(GMa₀)/R` and contradicts the derivative it had just written.** Verified numerically: B gives `a_r = +1.265473×10⁻¹⁴`, **MOND repulsive**, against the target `−1.265740×10⁻¹⁴`. **And it half-noticed** — its *“Wait — that sign comparison is important”* paragraph opens the right question and closes it wrongly. **Second time this family has dropped a sign on the logarithmic term without catching it** (#77 §7: it reverse-engineered `Σ_H = −k\ln R` from an equation giving `+k\ln R`). **Twice, same term, same direction, both times with a confident boxed answer.** The seed pre-registered the `d(ln R)/dR` versus `d(1/R)/dR` opposition as the whole content of the question; **it is also, empirically, the whole content of the failure — the strongest evidence yet that #71 was worth running numerically rather than reasoning out.** **A finding beyond the score:** Claude noted unprompted that the exercise **adjudicates the `Coh` contradiction** — `Coh = X` is `Paper_PhaseCoherence_P12Coh` §2's interference-only reading, while §5's *“`Coh` read as `|ΣP|²`”* would fold `D` in and give a wrong result. **So this morning's §5 correction, made as an internal judgement, now has independent outside backing reached from the other direction.** **What it does not establish:** the candidate list, the target and the framing are all mine, **so a family agreeing here is agreeing inside my setup.** The blind run remains the harder test and its S2 was 0/3. `substrate-evaluation/Seed_F1_S2_ThreeAssignments.md`.
77. **Blind `Σ_C` replication run 2026-09-05 — the collision was findable (2/2), the resolution was not (0/2), `Grad`'s Dirichlet form was independently proposed, and #71's sign check gets a scope correction.** **Two families (one returned the prompt rather than a reply; recorded as no-return). Blind: the prompt carried the corpus as it stood this morning, collision intact, with no hint that a problem existed.** **S1 passes 2/2 — both found the `Coh`/`Str` collision on one pass.** It took me a session and an external prompt. **S2 fails 0/2, and that is the result that matters.** **One family stated the stakes better than I did.** It computed `Coh − Str = −Σ_a b_a` exactly under the §2 reading and drew the consequence: *“phase/polarity — the substrate's supposedly primitive angular variable — plays no role at all in `Σ_C` … **decoherence events are gravitationally inert**, which seems like a strange design choice for a framework that puts phase transport among its primitives.”* **#70 framed the collision as the gravity arc losing MOND; the sharper framing is that P09 and P05 become dynamically irrelevant** — a structural indictment rather than an arc-local one. **Neither reached the settled split, and one refused to try** — correctly, under the seed's own honesty instruction: *“I am not going to pick the one that looks more likely to be right.”* The other proposed dropping `Str` entirely, **which loses Newton, and its own Newtonian check silently used the `−Str` its resolution deletes.** **So the pre-registered negative check does not fire — no family produced a rival resolution that reproduces the established results.** **But 0/2 is informative:** the settlement comes only from pushing three candidates through to `a(R)` and keeping the survivor, **and neither family ran that check even with the target in front of it.** **The split is not the obvious reading of the material; It is now the only wholly undefined term in `Σ_C`, and whether it should read commitment density, bandwidth, or participation amplitude is settled nowhere — **the three give different dynamics.** **The two `Str`s downgrade from contradiction to a naming collision the corpus already half-knew.** `../event-density/theory/P12_Coherence_PhaseAlignment_Scoping.md` Step 1 calls the certified `Coh = −(ρ−ρ_*)²` *“a density-target, not a coherence measure; **arguably mislabeled**”*, and records that the simulator is *“single-rule-type; no explicit polarity”* — a **tractability reduction**. **The corpus knew its simulator's Σ-terms are named after theory terms they do not implement; it was written nowhere a reader would meet it.** Naming notes now placed in `Paper_BlindnessInvariant` §2. **And the real problem, which is none of the three I was sent to fix.** **`|ΣP|²` is assigned to `Coh` *and* to `Str`, and they enter `Σ_C` with opposite signs.** `Paper_PhaseCoherence_P12Coh` gives `Coh` two different values three pages apart — §2's interference part `|ΣP|² − Σ|P|²` and §5's full square `|ΣP|²` — and the full square is what `P-Quadratic-Strain` assigns to `Str_K`. **On either reading the cross term `2√(b^{(L)}b^{(H)})·cos Θ_LH` cancels exactly, and that cross term is the entire MOND mechanism of `Paper_QuadraticStrain_v1` and `Paper_030`.** **Three candidate resolutions stated, none adopted** — the likeliest is that the two sums run over **disjoint index sets**, which **neither paper states**, which is precisely why nobody saw the collision. **Now Foundations #10, the heaviest open item in that arc.** **Research target #24(a) needs correcting and I am the one who mis-filed it.** I listed the `cos Θ` sign as **open**. `Paper_QuadraticStrain_v1` §9 has carried *“Update 2026-07-08 (constructive sign supplied)”* for two months, pointing at `Paper_PhaseCoherence_P12Coh`. **The sign was discharged in July.** What was stale is that paper's **audit table row 11**, still reading `A / OPEN` against its own §9 — a table-versus-body desync, now fixed. **So the celebrated arm-B finding —** *“an outside model found `Paper_QuadraticStrain_v1` §9's own residual”* **— is smaller than it read: it found a residual the same section had already answered.** The model was reading the postulate, not the paper, so it could not have known; It asks them to show whether GR is actually an output”*; **Gemini** wanted the substrate-strain-to-Ricci coupling; **Claude** wanted a **Jacobson-type thermodynamic closure** — entropy functional over participation configurations, temperature from `a_C` via Unruh, Clausius across substrate horizons. **`Paper_GravityAsEquationOfState` and `Paper_KhronometricEquationOfState_Jacobson` are that route, already in the corpus.** **A model told nothing about ED recommended, as its single highest-value request, the strategy the corpus actually took.** That is the closest any arm has come to an outside reader independently choosing ED's approach. **Y3 convergence, three of three:** `V5`'s functional form is under-specified so **no `1/r` follows** — *“bounded, decaying, retarded is insufficient to determine its infrared Green function”* — which **diagnoses the T2 wall arms A and B merely hit**; the **matter-sources-geometry step is missing and is the one that matters**, *“not a small missing lemma; it is the entire content of ‘why is there gravity’”*; and **`D01` is unusable**, again. **Two of three:** universal free fall not established (now Foundations #9), and **`D06` unusable at label-plus-role granularity** — **Gemini alone took it on faith and derived from it**, the cleanest illustration in three arms of what that family does differently. **The single disagreement is more useful than the agreements.** The `(iii) genuine postulate` versus `(iv) hard derivation` call was the arm's design purpose, and the families agree everywhere except one place: **two of three say the gravitational field equation needs a postulate; GPT alone holds it open as possibly derivable** — *“it is conceivable that the Einstein-Hilbert action could emerge without being explicitly assumed”* — and **names its own falsification condition**: *“I would change this from hard derivation to genuine postulate if the authors can show their microscopic construction has no mechanism capable of selecting the Einstein-Hilbert term.”* **That is a better statement of ED's open question than the corpus's own.** Not *“can we derive GR?”* but ***“does the substrate contain a mechanism that selects Einstein-Hilbert over `f(R)` and the rest?”*** — **nothing in the corpus asks it that way, and it is answerable.** **The heaviest finding is Foundations #8 and it came from GPT's opening paragraph.** `P12` names `Coh`, `Str` and `Grad`; **only `Str` has a definition, so `Σ_C` cannot be evaluated and `a_C` cannot be calculated.** Verified across `Paper_087`/`088`/`026`/`027`/`054`. **And the one paper that does define them — `Paper_BlindnessInvariant_KnotsNotCrystals` §2 — gives `Str = ρ_v`, a local commitment density, while `P-Quadratic-Strain` gives `Str_K = |Σ_a P_K^{(a)}|²`. Two incompatible definitions of the same symbol, one per arc, reconciled nowhere — and every gravitational result in the corpus runs through `a_C = −∇_adj Σ_C`.** **Two structural declarations, both checked before scoring, and the checking changed one of them.** Universal free fall was filed as a *contradiction*; `Paper_MassWithoutMass_BindingInertia`'s measured inertia (`v_x = 0.72` bound vs `0.97` unbound) **weakens it to *unproven***, and that paper's own item 4 already concedes the equivalence-principle reading is *“a consistent interpretation, not an independent proof.”* **GPT's second declaration sharpened Foundations #6:** it states the `V5` Lorentz circularity explicitly — *“Lorentz structure → V5 → emergent Lorentz structure”* — **and supplies the repair the corpus's own statement lacks:** distinguish a primitive discrete invariant from the emergent macroscopic metric. Claude's Gap 1 supplies machinery for the primitive side (Poisson sprinkling at `ℓ_ED⁻⁴`, a `(iii)` postulate making statistical Lorentz invariance a solved `(iv)` derivation). **What this settles about the protocol.** **Arms A and B asked the wrong question** — *“can a model generate ED?”* had a known answer, and both negatives' value came out sideways. **Arm C asked for that material directly and got it in one pass.** **The assumption-economy measure that dominated A and B should stay retired:** counting assumptions rewarded the family that assumed the hard part, while **counting gaps rewards the family that read carefully**, and the ordering matches engagement depth across all three arms. **Standing recommendation: run arm C's question on any arc the corpus wants audited from outside** — stated assumptions in, where-it-stops-and-what-it-needs out, with the postulate-versus-derivation call required. One paste per family.
68. **Target #18 worked 2026-09-05 — the `2π`'s load-bearing sentence is replaced by a primitive, and the debt moves down one level.** **What §4b owed was one sentence:** *why is the comparison made at the chain's layer rather than a common layer?* It was answered with a plausibility argument. **It now has a hook in a primitive.** `Paper_087` §P12's operational content is `a_C = −∇_adj Σ_C`, *“along its adjacency direction”* — **a difference over substrate-graph adjacency, not a continuum gradient. So in ED an acceleration is definitionally a layer-1 object, and there is no second ED definition at layer 2.** **And P12 is listed in `Paper_087` as load-bearing in `Paper_029` itself**, so the route appeals only to a commitment `a₀`'s own paper already declares. **The asymmetry stops being a choice:** the chain's side is layer 1 by P12, the horizon's side is coarse-grained by `Paper_028` §3.4's statistical surface, and neither is selected. **One correction to §4b's** *“solid”* **half, found while doing this.** It says the cosmic surface *“**cannot** contribute a `κ`”* and then writes `T_H = κ_H/(2π) = H₀/(2π)`, **which requires one** — and the de Sitter result it imports is derived from exactly the geometric structure the sentence denies. **Repair is one qualifier and it strengthens the route:** no **layer-1** `κ`, which is what §3.4 actually establishes, while at layer 2 it coarse-grains to `κ_H = H₀`. **The layer asymmetry then appears on both sides explicitly instead of being asserted on one.** **What is still owed, and it is narrower.** That the horizon's coarse-grained state contributes `T_H` and **not `2π T_H`** to a layer-1 adjacency gradient. **The layer argument establishes that one conversion factor sits between the sides; it does not establish the direction.** Both readings are dimensionally legal and differ by `(2π)² ≈ 39`. **So the debt moved down a level rather than being paid** — from a question about the framework's philosophy to a question about a single sign in an exponent, which is the kind a construction settles. **Tier unchanged: `1/(2π)` remains Postulated / disputed and `Paper_029`'s banner stands.** Recorded in `Note_a0_TwoPi_RepairRoutes.md` §4f. **Explicitly not banked** — the day's pattern of claiming a step and withdrawing it applies here with full force.
67. **Consilience passes re-checked 2026-09-05 — `F-EXIST` was an outlier, not a pattern; two stale rows and one definitional slip fixed.** **Swept on the suspicion that #66's `F-EXIST` was a pattern. It is not — and that is the finding.** Every marker checked: `consilience pass`, `already observed`, `already cleared`, `postdiction`, across `ED_Master_Predictions_List`, `Paper_101_FalsificationRegister`, `gravity/Predictions_and_Falsifiers`, `Paper_ED_Postdictions_PassedTests`. **The corpus's discipline here is good and in places exemplary.** **What holds, and it is most of it.** **Tier 1 is correctly framed as a floor, not a pitch:** *“These are known facts the substrate reproduces … **not new predictions**, but real constraints the framework had to clear.”* **`Paper_101` §0.3 is a dedicated section titled** *“The postdiction column — consilience, NOT weapons (keep separate)”*, naming several as shared with ΛCDM/MOND/GR. **BTFR (1.4) was softened 2026-07-14 against SPARC** with the measured slope `3.85 ± 0.09` printed next to ED's exact 4 — **a model of the practice.** **UDM is flagged everywhere** as a self-published preprint and a postdiction. **Light bending and GW speed are honest**: both shared with GR, both stated as bars ED had to clear rather than wins. **Two rows had standing findings that never reached them, and both are in `gravity/Predictions_and_Falsifiers.md` — the arc's public-facing falsification map.** **(1) The `G` row** sits under *“Postdictions already on the table”* while **Staleness #26 records the arc's own verdict that** *“`G` was not explained, it was renamed”* — a judgment F1 arm B reproduced independently. The row's parenthetical is accurate; the heading over it is not. **(2) The `a₀` row** still reads *“parameter-free”* although the `2π` has been **Postulated / disputed since 2026-09-04**, and does not note that **the relation including the `2π` is prior art** (#60). **Both fixed.** Item 21 again, and again the public-facing document was the one left stale. **One definitional slip, in the outreach paper, and it is the kind a hostile reader checks first.** `Paper_ED_Postdictions_PassedTests` §1 defines *“passed tests are predictions made **before** the confirming data”* and then classifies **GW170817 as a passed test** — but the observation is 2017 and `Paper_ED_GW_00` postdates it. **By the paper's own definition it is a postdiction.** Re-labelled to what is actually true and is a real claim: **a survival result** — GW170817 eliminated a class of modified-gravity programs and ED is not in that class. **Consequence stated plainly in the paper: the corpus has *zero* passed tests in the strict sense, and every ED-distinctive bet is still ahead of its data.** **Why `F-EXIST` was an outlier rather than a pattern, which is worth understanding.** The consilience passes audited here are all about **whether a known result is ED-distinctive** — a question answerable from inside the corpus, and the corpus answers it well. **`F-EXIST` was different in kind: it asserted that an experimental phenomenon (MIR saturation) occurs, and it does not occur in the materials that arc names.** That is checklist item 22's inward direction, and **no amount of internal discipline reaches it.** The distinction is the useful output of this sweep: **“is this ours?” is checkable internally; “does this happen?” is not.**
66. **`a_eff` settled 2026-09-05 — three lengths under one symbol; the P08→DCGT chain does not hold; `F-EXIST` has fired.** **Settled, and #65's dilemma collapses onto its second horn far harder than that entry put it.** `a_eff` is **three physically distinct lengths under one symbol**, and the symbol appears **only** in the two transport papers — **not once in `Paper_073_DCGT`, which they cite as its source.** **(1)** The mechanism's own words derive it from **P08**, whose floor is `ℓ_ED ≈ 1.6×10⁻³⁵ m`. **(2)** §9.2 calls it *“the DCGT cell”*, `R_cg`. **(3)** Every number the arc computes uses the interatomic spacing, `≈ 3×10⁻¹⁰ m`. **Twenty-five orders of magnitude between the first and the third.** **This is checklist item 19 in its purest form — follow the quantity, not the spelling** — and it survived because each section's local use was reasonable. **The DCGT reading is not available, and DCGT rules it out itself.** §3.1: `R_cg` is *“chosen anywhere in the scale-separated window”* and coarse-grained quantities are *“insensitive to the specific choice.”* **A chosen scale to which results are insensitive cannot be a physical floor** — a mean free path floored at `R_cg` would make a measurable quantity depend on where an analyst decided to average. **Calling `a_eff` the DCGT cell makes the arc's central mechanism an artifact of bookkeeping.** **And the P08 chain has an unwritten step.** The stated route is: the substrate supports nothing finer than one hop (P08), *“DCGT-coarse-grained to the transport scale”*, therefore the mean free path floors at the cell. **Coarse-graining a floor at `ℓ_ED` does not install a floor at `R_cg`** — averaging removes structure below the cell, it does not erect a barrier at it. **The entire load is carried by one asserted sentence**, *“In ED that lattice spacing **is** the effective transport cell `a_eff`”*, **and both P08 and DCGT appear in the chain while doing no work in it.** **`F-EXIST` has fired, and it had been recorded as passed on half the literature.** The falsifier reads: *“charge transport that continues rising without any saturation, with the mean free path driven below `a_eff` under control of confounds.”* **That is the documented behaviour of bad metals** (Gunnarsson, Calandra & Han, RMP **75**, 1085: high-T꜀ cuprates and alkali-doped fullerides violate Ioffe–Regel). **The parenthetical is the tell** — *“MIR saturation is already observed, so this is a consilience pass, not a new bet”* — the paper checked its falsifier against **only the confirming half** of the literature. **A falsifier checked that way has not been checked**, and the same reading should be assumed of any other falsifier in the corpus marked passed by consilience. **Forked honestly, and both branches cost the arc its target regime.** **(A)** If the mean free path is a real length past Ioffe–Regel, `P-Scattering-Budget-Saturation` is **falsified in cuprates and fullerides**. **(B)** If it is not — the standard reading, and the one behind #65's §3 finding — then F-EXIST cannot fire **but neither can the mechanism apply**, since *“the mean free path is floored at the cell”* is then a statement about nothing in that regime; the arc must retreat to materials that saturate, where no electron viscosity is measured, **leaving F-COONSET with no venue.** **(B) is the more likely physics and the worse outcome**, being a loss of purchase rather than a refutation one could argue with. **The settlement, and it is a narrowing rather than a demolition.** `a_eff` is **the material's own emergent transport cell, inherited** — the resistivity paper already half-said so, writing *“an **(emergent)** transport cell”*. **P08 and DCGT leave the chain. `ρ_max` re-tiered from** *“identification / consilience”* **to plain `I`**, since the former read as partial credit for grounding MIR in P04. **ED does not explain MIR; it inherits it.** **What survives is exactly the arc's distinctive content:** momentum and charge draw on **one** budget at that cell. **And `Λ` survives untouched**, because it cancels `a_eff` whatever `a_eff` is — its separate domain-of-validity defect (#65) is unaffected and still open. **What would reverse this:** a substrate argument that the emergent transport cell is fixed by something in ED rather than read off the material. **Nothing in the corpus attempts one**, and `Paper_073_DCGT` §7.2 records DCGT breaking down as `R_cg → ℓ_ED`, which is the direction such an argument would have to travel. **Until one exists, `a_eff` is `I` everywhere it appears.** **Pattern note for the checklist.** Two days, two arcs, same shape: a symbol whose meaning drifted between sections while every local use looked fine, load-bearing at the join. **The `a₀` case was caught by an outside model; this one by following a citation to a paper that turned out not to contain the symbol.** **Reading a cited source for the definition it is cited for is the cheapest audit in the corpus and it is not on any checklist.** Added as item 24.
65. **F-CONVERGE run 2026-09-05 — not runnable; and the attempt found that the arc's wall may not exist in the regime it targets, plus a domain-of-validity defect in §9.** **Ran on instruction. It does not run, and finding out why cost the arc more than the test would have won.** `ℓ_ee` and `ℓ_mr` are measured in **disjoint** windows in every established system — WP₂ is hydrodynamic below ~20 K and conventional above; graphene and PdCoO₂ are ultra-clean low-temperature systems — **so there is no dataset to run it against.** Full write-up: `soft-matter/Note_FConverge_Run_2026-09-05.md`. **Finding 1, and it is about the wall rather than the test. Bad metals do not saturate.** §3 reads `ρ_max` as the **MIR saturation value** and §8 makes strange metals the arena. **Gunnarsson, Calandra & Han (Rev. Mod. Phys. 75, 1085) name high-T꜀ cuprates and alkali-doped fullerides as violating the Ioffe–Regel condition** — the extracted mean free path passes *below* the interatomic spacing with no saturation at all. **The arc points at the one material family that has no wall.** Where saturation *is* observed, electron viscosity is not measured, so the co-onset cannot be looked for there either. **Stated as a dilemma, not a refutation, because both horns cost something:** if `a_eff` is the interatomic spacing the floor is violated and the wall is not where the arc says; if it is not, then MIR saturation is not ED's wall and §3's identification of `ρ_max` — with §8's corrected pairing built on it — is a misidentification. **Either way the arc owes an argument that is currently carried by a name.** **Finding 2: the one candidate venue is awkward, and the paper does not consider it.** The Dirac fluid in charge-neutral graphene reaches a momentum-transport bound and quantum-critical dissipation together, at room temperature — Ku et al., Nature 583, 537, report viscosity and scattering rates *“comparable to the universal values expected at quantum criticality.”* **Momentum transport is at or near its floor there; charge transport is not**, and that separation is what makes the system hydrodynamic. **On the arc's own account `f → 1` is one event, so this is a tension with F-COONSET, not with F-CONVERGE.** **Not banked**, because three escapes are real: *“comparable to”* is not *“at”*; co-onset is explicitly conditional on *“one cell governing both transports”* and at charge neutrality momentum relaxation is extrinsic; and there is **no Fermi surface**, so `p_F` is undefined and `Λ` does not apply. **The second escape has a price:** it says the test needs a material that genuinely saturates, which by Finding 1 means one with no measured viscosity. **The two conditions have never been met together and it is not obvious any known material meets them.** **Finding 3, the consequential one, and it is against yesterday's §9 — mine.** §9.2 obtains `Π_q = 1` from Drude and `Π_p = 1/3` from kinetic theory, **both evaluated at `ℓ = a_eff`, the wall — exactly where the literature says the quasiparticle description stops holding.** **Both inherited ingredients of `Λ` are used outside their domain of validity, at the one point where the answer is wanted.** §9.5's `I` tier and §9.6's `[1/5, 1/3]` band do **not** cover this: the problem is not coefficient uncertainty but that at the wall `p_F` and `n` may not be the right variables — in which case **`Λ`'s parameter-independence, which §9.6 calls the whole claim, loses the derivation that produced it.** `Λ` may still be right, since bounds of that shape are reached without quasiparticles in the Planckian and holographic literature, **but ED has not derived it that way.** **Cheapest repair, and it should be done: restrict `Λ` to the quasiparticle regime *approaching* the wall rather than at it** — honest, testable, and it turns `Λ` into a limiting statement. **A claim of mine withdrawn, at every site carrying it.** On 2026-09-05 I recorded F-CONVERGE as *“the cheapest test in the arc”* and *“the actionable item; the derivation is done.”* **That asserted an availability I had not checked**, and the recommendation to run it was made on that basis. Corrected in §6's falsifier list, §9.8, ML 4.11 and target #23. **ML 4.11 is re-marked scoped rather than live.** **The methodological point, and it is the day's cleanest instance.** Four instruments were built today and none of them would have caught any of this: **all three findings required leaving the corpus.** Finding 1 is failure mode 8 turned inward — not a false claim *about* a neighbouring literature but **an unchecked assumption that a neighbouring literature's phenomenon exists in the form ED needs.** **Checklist item 22 should be read as covering both directions**, and its instance list now includes an arc built on a wall that the target materials do not have. **Held to the standing bar on negatives.** Every finding here is recorded with its escape named, none is called a falsification, and §4 of the note states what would settle each. **The corpus's own caution applies with full force in the direction that flatters nobody: this is a negative about ED, produced in one session, and it should be re-checked before it is leaned on.**

(`Paper_114_GRH_D1_P_Gauge`) is a verdict-*upgrade* condition, not a falsifier at all** — deriving `P-Gauge` from primitives moves the paper **M2 → M1**, and nothing about the world decides it.
