(`Paper_027`) was reformulated 2026-09-04 precisely because it could not fire**: it needs two independent determinations of `ℓ_ED` and the corpus has one. **A theory-internal condition is a good thing for a paper to declare and a bad thing to advertise as a bet**, and the corpus had no vocabulary separating the two. **The ten, placed to mirror the papers rather than lumped.** **ML 4.10** gains **`F-R1`** — its content *is* that row's mechanism (two pure-Class-C architectures plateauing at different correlation content). **New ML 4.12** (`F-QD-1`…`F-QD-4`): the budget bifurcates on P11 commitment, so **a committed record costs no V5 budget** and accumulated classical redundancy is unbounded while live correlation is capped. **New ML 4.13** (`F-LIVE`, `F-SAT`, `F-CROSSTALK`): the V5 photonic-routing fifth face. **New ML 4.14** (`F-D2`, `F-D3`): the proxy-conversion doctrine. **Two of the new rows carry warnings that matter more than the bets.** **4.13 is listed as NOT a live bet:** `F-LIVE` is make-or-break and **unestablished** — nobody has shown that routing preserves correlation uncommitted rather than recording a classical which-band tag, and **if the routing commits, ED predicts no V5 ceiling for the device at all and the row is void.** It is in the list so the gate is visible in the inventory instead of only in the paper. **4.14 is methodological**, and its leverage is inverted: a `F-D2`/`F-D3` failure **would invalidate the reading of 4.10–4.13 rather than any one of them.** That is why a doctrine paper with *“no new physics claims”* belongs in a predictions list. **`F-QD-3` is the one to respect and it is now visible.** It falsifies on *“observed saturation of **accumulated** classical redundancy in a growing environment”* — which **runs against the naive reading of its own arc, deliberately.** A framework whose falsifiers all point away from itself is not being tested, and this one had been sitting unlisted. **Script hardened so the clearance does not rot.** The exemptions live in a `NOT_A_PREDICTION` dict with stated reasons rather than resting on the names happening to appear in a prose note — **an unexplained exemption is how a check quietly stops meaning anything.** Baseline re-taken at **0 orphans, 0 dangling citations, 0 distinctive-but-uncited, 3 declared exemptions**, and **re-verified from the empty baseline**: a planted `F-NEWBET` was caught as newly orphaned with exit 1, its removal returned exit 0. **From here any new named falsifier that never reaches the inventory trips the check on the next run.** **What the day's five instruments now cover.** Failure mode 8 (comparative claims) needs a human and a search box. Item 21 (propagation) is now partly mechanical: the postulate census, the comparative-claim sweep, the transport-invariant check, and this cross-check. **The predictions inventory is, for the first time, navigable from the papers in both directions.**
63. **Predictions cross-check built into the census script 2026-09-05 — 19 of 19 named falsifiers were orphaned; ED's dark-matter bets were missing from ED's own prediction list.** **Built into `internal notes/_census_postulates.py`, which now carries two jobs and fails on drift in either.** Three checks: **P1** named falsifiers (`F-NAME`) the master predictions list never mentions; **P2** master-list paper citations resolving to no file; **P3** papers whose own prose calls their falsifier content *distinctive* or *ED-owned* which the list does not cite. Detail with `--predictions`; the summary runs unconditionally, because **the failure it catches is silent by construction and must not sit behind a flag.** **The design decision that made it work, after a first attempt that did not.** Keying on *any* paper with a falsification section gave **210 papers, 180 of them absent from the list** — noise, because the list is a curated superset of the sharp bets, not an index. And `F1`/`F2` numbering is per-paper and non-unique (600+ uses), so it cannot join anything. **But a paper *names* a falsifier exactly when it is specific enough to be a bet.** That makes `F-NAME` the natural join key, and P1 enforces it as one. **Precision went from 17% to 100%.** **First run: 19 of 19 named falsifiers orphaned.** Not one of the corpus's specifically-named falsifiers appeared in its own prediction inventory. **The join-key convention existed as a habit, never as a discipline**, and that is exactly why the transport arc could stay invisible: **the master list cannot be navigated from the papers.** Baselined as a *set* rather than a count, so fixing one and adding another is still caught. **P3 earned its keep on the first run, and its find is worse than the one that prompted the check.** `Paper_ED_DarkSector` §6 is titled *“The verdict: the distinctive bets and the honest ledger”* and lists among them **“dark matter is warm (~keV), not a cold WIMP”** and **“galaxies contain no dark-matter halo at all.”** **Neither was in `ED_Master_Predictions_List.md`. A grep for `warm`, `keV`, `WIMP` and `halo` returned zero hits.** **ED's most direct-detection-facing bet had no row in ED's own prediction inventory.** Now **ML 1.17 and 1.18**, with the two-culprit exposure stated: a cold-halo detection inside galaxies refutes both at once. **Backlog worked down 19 → 13 in the same session, and P3 to zero.** `F-COONSET`, `F-BAND`, `F-RATIO`, `F-CONVERGE` cleared by putting the labels into ML 4.11; `F-EXIST`, `F-R2` by citing the companion `Paper_P04TransportBudget_ResistivitySaturation`. **The remaining 13 are a real to-do, not a script artifact** — each is a named bet with no navigable row: `F-BC` (Paper_030), `F-P` (Paper_027), `F-R1`, `F-SAT`, `F-LIVE`, `F-CROSSTALK` (V5 papers), `F-D2`/`F-D3` (ProxyConversionDoctrine), `F-QD-1..4` (QuantumDarwinism), `F-Gauge` (Paper_114). **Cleared by adding the label to the relevant master-list row, never by deleting it from the paper.** **Verified end to end in both directions**, which matters for a tripwire nobody will look at again for months: a planted `F-DRIFTTEST` label was detected as newly orphaned and the script exited 1; removing it returned exit 0; the four genuine resolutions were reported as resolved and `--update` re-baselined the set. **Both the postulate census and the predictions check now share one exit code**, so a single run after any session says which of the two authoritative lists has gone stale. **What this closes and what it does not.** It closes the one member of the day's defect family a machine can catch — **item 21's third face, an inventory as a propagation target.** It does **not** touch failure mode 8: a comparative claim about another literature still needs a human with a search box, and no script will ever flag one. **Two defect classes, two instruments, and only one of them can be automated.**
62. **`Π_p`/`Π_q` derived 2026-09-05 — the transport-wall invariant is the **product**, `Λ = η_minρ_max e²/p_F² = Π_pΠ_q`; the arc's R2-analog closes in form, and §8's ratio claim is corrected.** **Run on instruction directly after #61, which is what made it tractable.** The correction there relocated the discriminator from `φ_max : ρ_max` to `η_min : ρ_max`; both of those are floored at one mean free path, and once that is granted the algebra is textbook. **The arc's R2-analog, open since the paper was written, closes in form.** **Result.** `Π_p = 1/3` (kinetic shear viscosity at the floor `η = ⅓np_Fℓ`), `Π_q = 1` (Drude at the floor `ρ = p_F/ne²ℓ`), giving **`Λ ≡ η_minρ_max e²/p_F² = Π_pΠ_q`** with **`n`, `a_eff`, `b_adj^max` and the coupling all cancelling.** Equivalently `η_minρ_max = (ħk_F²/6π)·R_K` — **the product of the two transport walls is the resistance quantum times a purely geometric carrier factor.** Checked numerically in three independent forms plus an `(n, a_eff)`-independence sweep: `internal notes/_check_transport_invariant.py`, re-runnable, all three agreeing to twelve digits. **Two corrections were needed to get there, and the second was inside the paper's own most recent update.** §8 (2026-07-30) had already fixed the pairing. It then asserted *“their ratio `η_min : ρ_max` is an O(1) that cancels `a_eff`.”* **It does not.** `η_min ∝ a_eff` and `ρ_max ∝ 1/a_eff`, so the ratio scales as `a_eff²` and **the product is the invariant. F-RATIO had been written against the ratio and was untestable as stated.** *The general lesson: same-kind does not mean same-sign — one floor can enter two transport coefficients with opposite powers, and “they share a length so the ratio is clean” is not an argument.* **The most useful output is not the number.** The `a_eff` cancellation exists **only** under a single floor; with two, the algebra returns `Λ = Π_pΠ_q·(a_η/a_ρ)`. **So `Λ` measures `P-Adjacency-Transport-Shared` directly**, rather than testing against a computed number — a strictly better falsifier than the one the paper was reaching for. **F-RATIO restated, and its sharper half is the drift test:** `Λ` must not track lattice spacing, carrier density or material, **and that test does not depend on resolving the coefficient band.** **F-CONVERGE, new, and the cheapest test the arc has.** Standard treatments keep the momentum-conserving and momentum-relaxing lengths distinct — electron hydrodynamics is *defined* by `ℓ_ee ≪ ℓ_mr`, established by orders of magnitude in graphene, PdCoO₂, WP₂. **ED does not contradict that** (its claim is about the wall, where both floor at the cell) **but it does require the two to converge on approach.** A system driven to MIR saturation with `ℓ_ee` and `ℓ_mr` still orders apart falsifies the posit **with no viscosity measurement at all**, which matters because the electron-fluid viscosity is the hard half to measure. **Tier held down deliberately: `D-via-I`, not substrate-native.** The ED content is one sentence — at the wall the two mean free paths are the same length because they are the same band — and inherited kinetic theory supplies the rest. **The `1/3` is a kinetic-averaging convention, not a forced number:** the degenerate-Fermi-gas result gives `1/5`, and at the wall, where quasiparticles are marginal, neither scheme is clearly right. **`Λ ∈ [1/5, 1/3]`, an inherited ambiguity not closable from the substrate**, so the claim is the *existence and parameter-independence* of `Λ`, never its value. **This is the discipline #61 was written about, applied to the paper #61 corrected**, on the same day. **Prior-art check run before writing, per the new checklist item 22.** The `η`–`ρ` link is not virgin ground: **Lucas & Hartnoll, PNAS 114, 11344 (2017)** bound the resistivity of hydrodynamic bad metals, and in the viscous-momentum-relaxation picture the resistivity contribution **rises** with `η` — **the opposite sign of correlation to `Λ = const`, which has `ρ ∝ 1/η`.** Regimes differ (inhomogeneity-driven relaxation versus a mean-free-path floor), so this is **not a contradiction**; but where both apply, **the sign of the `η`–`ρ` correlation discriminates**, which is a sharper handle than either framework's own bound. **One citation left unpinned on purpose:** the `ρ_DC ∼ η` proportionality is standard but is not in the PNAS abstract and was not verified against the body; §9.9 says *pin before submission* rather than inventing a section number. **Propagation failure caught in passing, and it is a bad one.** The transport arc's predictions — co-onset included, which the paper calls its *“distinctive, falsifiable, ED-owned content”* — **had never been entered in `ED_Master_Predictions_List.md` at all.** A distinctive falsifiable bet sat outside the corpus's own prediction inventory from the paper's writing until today. **Now ML 4.11**, filed under Quantum Foundations and Soft Matter with all three legs (co-onset, the `Λ` drift test, F-CONVERGE) and the Lucas–Hartnoll contrast. **Item 21's third face again: the inventory is a propagation target, and nothing had flagged the omission because nothing cross-checks arc papers against the master list.**
61. **Comparative-claim sweep 2026-09-05 — four external-literature claims in the corpus, three wrong or over-broad, all corrected.** **Ran on instruction after #60, across all 300 markdown files in `physics-papers/`, on thirteen pattern classes** (`no analogue`, `does not exist in`, `nobody / no one has`, `has never been`, `unique to ED`, `first to`, `no other framework`, `not been noted`, `nothing else`, `standard physics has no / lacks`, `MOND|ΛCDM|GR ... has no / lacks / cannot`, `genuinely novel / unprecedented / no counterpart`, `cannot be done in`). Script: `internal notes/_sweep_comparative_claims.py`, re-runnable. **56 raw hits.** Most are self-referential — ledger entries about the corpus's own history, or *“nobody had looked”* about ED's own material — and are not this defect class. **Four were genuine external-literature claims. Three were wrong or over-broad.** **(1) `Paper_UnifiedP04TransportBudget` §8 — FALSE, and it contradicted its own page.** It read *“Standard physics has no reason to unify them; ED forces it”* of the MIR resistivity ceiling and the KSS/Planckian viscosity floor. **Three lines above, the same paper correctly says condensed-matter physics “actively debates whether they are the same physics.”** Both cannot hold. The literature is decisive: **Zaanen (2019), *Planckian dissipation, minimal viscosity and the transport in cuprate strange metals*, SciPost Phys. 6, 061 (arXiv:1807.10951)** argues exactly this unification, with *“the momentum relaxation rate governing the resistivity relat[ing] directly to the electronic entropy.”* **ED is entering an open debate on one side, not drawing a link nobody had reason to draw.** Corrected claim: the hydrodynamic route reaches the link through minimal viscosity and entropy, ED through **one mean-free-path floor at `ℓ = a_eff` shared by charge and momentum** — different mechanisms, and **the discriminating quantity is `η_min : ρ_max`, which the paper leaves underived.** Until that ratio exists ED's position here is a stance, not a separating prediction. *(“ED forces it” also breached the no-forcing posture.)* **(2) `Paper_KhronometricEquationOfState_Jacobson` preamble — over-broad, narrowed.** *“Nobody — in ED or in the Einstein-aether literature — has actually written out Jacobson's local-Rindler `δQ = TdS` derivation for a khronometric theory.”* **Two problems.** A negative-existence claim about a literature **cannot be verified in principle**, so the honest form is *“we have not found”*. And it invites a reader to think of work it does not cover: **Einstein-aether horizon thermodynamics is a developed subfield** — **Berglund, Bhattacharyya & Mattingly, PRL 110, 071301 (2013)** establish a first law and an entropy for universal horizons. **That is a different object** (a global first law at a universal horizon, versus deriving field equations from a local Clausius relation on Rindler wedges) **and the claim must say so rather than leave it to the reader.** The underlying contribution survives; only its framing was wrong. **(3) `Paper_ED_DF2_DF4_GroupSuppression` Prediction 4 — narrowed, and its propagated copy in the cosmology ledger with it.** *“A uniquely ED prediction. No MOND-EFE variant naturally produces it.”* **Neither half holds.** MOND's field equation is **nonlinear**, so `g_ext` is itself sourced by the full external mass distribution rather than being an independent local parameter; **modified-inertia** formulations are non-local by construction; and the EFE differs quantitatively between formulations (stronger in AQUAL than QUMOND). **MOND does carry environment-structure dependence.** The live question is whether its *shape* differs from ED's cumulative-line-of-sight-commitment dependence — **and neither has been computed**, ED's suppression function being Open in its own ledger. **Downgraded from a uniqueness claim to a candidate discriminator.** **(4) `Paper_V5PhotonicRouting_FifthFace` — checked, and it stands.** *“That coupling has no counterpart in independent-channel loss”* is true by construction: independent-channel loss is defined by the absence of the coupling. **A definitional contrast, not a literature claim.** Recorded so a later sweep does not re-open it. **The standing rule this establishes, and it is the sweep's real output.** **A comparative claim about a neighbouring literature is the corpus's highest-risk sentence type.** Every other claim-strength defect is catchable by re-reading ED; these are catchable only from outside, so they survive every internal audit and propagate like verified facts — #60's instance reached the public workbook. **Two sub-rules.** **(a) Negative-existence claims are unverifiable in principle.** *“Nobody has”* is never the honest form; *“we have not found”* is. **(b) The narrow true form is almost always the stronger one**, because it names what ED actually adds. In all three corrections here, the honest version was sharper than the overclaim it replaced, and in (1) and (3) it converted a dead assertion into a **named, computable open item**. **Base rate for calibration.** Four genuine instances in 300 papers, three defective. **The claim type is rare and its defect rate is high** — which is the right shape for a checklist item rather than a standing audit: cheap to scan for, expensive to leave.
60. **Prior-art repair on `a₀ ≈ cH₀/(2π)`, 2026-09-05 — acting on #59 surfaced a false claim about the MOND literature that had propagated to five sites, including the public workbook.** **Fixed — and the fix found a worse defect one file over.** Acting on #59 meant checking what the MOND literature actually says. Three things came back. **(1)** `a₀ ∼ cH₀` is Milgrom's, from 1983. **(2)** Milgrom (2020), *The `a₀` – cosmology connection in MOND* (arXiv:2001.09729), is **a review devoted entirely to this coincidence**, stating it as `a₀ ~ cH₀ ~ c²Λ^{1/2} ~ c²/ℓ_U` and calling it *“a potentially crucial aspect of MOND.”* **(3) The `2π`-normalized form `a₀ ≈ cH₀/(2π)` is itself standard in the review literature** (Famaey & McGaugh 2012). **So the `2π` is prior art as well.** `Paper_029`'s framing — `cH₀` known, `2π` ED's contribution — **was wrong on both halves.** What is genuinely not in the literature is a *derivation* of the coefficient, and that is now what the paper claims. **The serious find: `Paper_ED_Pred_H0_From_a0.md` §1 rested on a false premise about MOND.** It read *“This probe does not exist in MOND. There `a₀` is a constant of nature with no connection to cosmology; there is nothing to invert.”* **Milgrom's 2020 review is that connection.** The statement would have been caught on sight by any MOND-literate referee, and it was **prediction 1.16's entire novelty argument**. **Corrected claim, which is sharper than the overclaim:** MOND has the relation but **no derived normalization**, and an order-of-magnitude relation inverts only to an order-of-magnitude `H₀`, which measures nothing. **ED's distinctiveness is the coefficient.** That places the probe's whole novelty on the `2π` — which is **Postulated/disputed** (#10). **The probe's novelty and the arc's weakest link are the same object**, and the paper now says so. **Propagation: five sites, one origin, five weeks.** The “no MOND analogue” claim was written once on 2026-09-04 and copied into `ED_Master_Predictions_List.md` ML 1.16, this ledger's own #40, `Paper_ED_Pred_H0_From_a0.md` §1 **and** its audit table row 3, and **`ED_ItemizedTheory_TieredClaims.xlsx` `Core Predictions` C19** — the sheet the public PDF is built from. **All five corrected.** Audit-table row 3 downgraded **D-via-I → I**: the row derived a conclusion from a false premise, so its tier was never earned. **This is failure mode 7 and item 21 again, and the eighth instance in two days.** The pattern is stable enough to state as a rule: **a comparative claim about a neighbouring literature is the highest-risk sentence type in the corpus**, because nothing internal can check it and it propagates like any other. **Repairs to `Paper_029`.** New **§1.2.1** states the prior art with citations and locates ED's contribution against it (a mechanism, and a coefficient in place of the literature's `∼`); new **§1.2.2** carries the ED claim with its §8.4 dispute attached; **§6.2** no longer lists the *relation* `a₀ = cH₀/(2π)` as an ED structural output, only its derivation; **§6.3**'s wedge restated as *the absence of a mechanism, not the absence of the observation*; new **§8.9**; a **References** section, the paper's first. **“Geometrically forced” removed** — it contradicted §8.4 on the same page and the corpus's standing no-forcing posture. `Paper_ED_Predictions_Bundle.md`'s *“forces the precise `1/(2π)`”* went with it. **One citation left unpinned, deliberately.** Famaey & McGaugh 2012 is verified as the source through which the `2π` form circulates, but the published text is paywalled and **the section number is not pinned**. Recorded in the References entry as *“pin it before submission”* rather than guessed. **A fabricated section number in a prior-art citation would be a worse defect than the omission this item set out to fix.** **Net.** #59 flagged a presentation defect and estimated it small. It was not: **the framing was wrong, the `2π` was prior art, and a downstream prediction's novelty claim was false and had propagated to the public workbook.** The finding originated with a model that had never seen the corpus and mentioned the coincidence only in passing, while **declining to use it.**
59. **F1 arm B run 2026-09-05 — the 38 lines do not close the gap; both targets fail identically to arm A; the runs land on the corpus's own open items.** **Arm B run on the same three clean families. Both targets FAIL again, identically to arm A. The 38 lines do not close the gap.** Seeding all 38 — constants as values, the fifteen domain-specific postulates, `P-Quadratic-Strain` with its full algebraic form — moved **neither** T1 nor T2. **The hypothesis that “the 38 lines build ED” is not supported for these two steps**; whatever closes them is in the *papers*, not the assumption list. **The assumption counts move in opposite directions and both are informative.** GPT 14→11 — because `G`, `a₀`, `Λ`, `H₀` are now inherited, *“the theory does not need to explain the numerical value of `G`”*. Claude **8→9**, because `D07` resolved none of the standing gaps while opening three new ones. **Gemini 3→3 with a byte-identical A1 in both arms** — it postulated `V5 → 1/r` twice and did not engage the added material, which is itself a result about that family. **`D07` is the one real gain, and it confirms the corpus's own architecture from outside.** Arm A's criticism was that P12 has no other-chain dependence (Foundations #5). Arm B's verdict on the same model: *“`D07` gives you a real (if underdetermined) mechanism for chains to affect each other, which the first document's P12 lacked entirely — the first place in either document where multi-chain dependence appears.”* **An uncontaminated reader, twice, independently reconstructed how ED is actually built:** P12 is a single-chain functional and `P-Quadratic-Strain` is what makes it an interaction. **And it found `Paper_QuadraticStrain_v1`'s own declared residual, from the postulate alone.** The `cos Θ` sign: *“nothing forces `Θ_ab ≈ 0` … D07 alone gives you something that could as easily look repulsive or oscillatory as attractive.”* **That is §9's open item, located by a model that had never seen §9.** It raises the residual's priority — the sign is not a loose end, it is the first thing an external reader reaches for. Two further gaps named that the paper does *not* state: chain-level `Str(C)` is never derived from channel-level `Str_K`, and **the postulate gives the cross-term no spatial dependence at all**. Both checkable; addendum added to the paper. **Two presentation findings, one of which should be fixed before anything public.** **(1)** `a₀ ≈ cH₀/(2π)` **is a long-noted coincidence in the MOND literature**, raised unprompted and **declined** by the model because the seed forbade assuming relations among constants. **`Paper_029` cites Milgrom 1983 and gives the coincidence one uncited clause** (*“recognized for decades as suggestive of a cosmological connection”*) **while framing `cH₀` as the known part and the `2π` as ED's contribution.** *(Narrowed 2026-09-05 from “nowhere acknowledges”, which was too strong — the clause is there. The defect is the missing citation and, more seriously, the framing: **the `2π`-normalized form is prior art too.** See #60.)* ED's claim is a *derivation*, which is different and legitimate — but omitting the prior observation reads as claiming it. **Flagged in `Paper_029`.** **(2)** `G = c³ℓ_P²/ħ` was noticed and correctly declined: *“a consistency check on inputs, not a derivation of anything new”* — **independently reproducing ≦26's** *“`G` was not explained, it was renamed.”* **`D06` draws a real objection the gravity arc must answer.** *“An acoustic metric is a fixed background sourced by the flow, not a metric that responds to bandwidth the way `g_μν` responds to `T_μν`. Analogue gravity models do not generically yield the Einstein field equations.”* It bears directly on whether the acoustic-metric guardrails can carry the metric-emergence load **all three arm-A runs named as the missing bridge**. **And `D01` was called unusable by two families independently** — *“an undefined term … a placeholder”* — corroborating ≦46's row-24 concerns from readers who knew none of them. **Caveat that limits the negative, and it is not cosmetic.** Three of fifteen postulates were seeded at label-plus-role only (`D06`, `D08`–`D10`), and one run objected that asserting them *“by role only”* amounts to *“asserting the solution to an open problem in constructive QFT without supplying it.”* **`D06` is load-bearing for the metric route, so a fair retest needs the six guardrails stated in full.** A failure attributable to those three is a failure of the seed, not of the 38 lines. **Net across both arms: the corpus's self-assessment is accurate.** Three independent readers with no access to ED converged on the soft spots ED's own ledgers already name — the `cos Θ` sign, the metric-emergence gap, `D01`'s weak specification, the `G` circularity. **They did not find anything the corpus does not already know about itself.** That is the strongest thing either arm established, and it was not what either arm was testing for.
58. **F1 arm A run 2026-09-05 — the wall replicates on clean ground; F1 does not fire; three external findings about ED, two verified.** **F1 arm A run on the three Stage-0-clean families. Neither target reached. F1 does not fire and the standing verdict is strengthened.** No run separated commitment-front advance from clock tick (**T1**), and none derived the `1/R` profile from a channel count (**T2**). **The wall sits exactly where every prior seeded run put it — now replicated on ground screened for contamination**, which is what the arm existed to establish. **T2's failures differ, and the differences are the result.** **GPT** analysed the gap precisely and declined it: *“3 spatial dimensions alone do not derive the inverse-square law”*, with the flux premise `4πr²g(r) = const` noted as absent from the primitives and listed as its own **A7**. **Gemini postulated the answer and labelled it** — its **A1** reads *“the cross-chain kernel `V5(r)` approaches a `1/r` spatial dependence at macro distances”*, which **is the postulate ED derives**. **Claude named the route and rejected it as unlicensed**: *“ambient bandwidth density falls off with the causal-graph analogue of area (using P06's `4πR²` spheres), giving inverse-square by a flux/conservation argument … it is an addition, not a consequence.”* **So the shape of the `N(R)` argument is findable, was found, and was correctly judged not to follow from the primitives as stated.** That is a sharper negative than ‘nobody thought of it’. **Assumption counts: 14 (GPT), 3 (Gemini), 8 (Claude), against a prior-best of 6 and ED's 4. The count must not be quoted alone.** **Gemini's 3 is fewer than ED's 4 and is not a better result** — it reached Newtonian gravity on three assumptions by assuming the hard parts wholesale (the `1/r` asymptotics, bandwidth-mass equivalence, metric coupling). **Counting postulates without weighting what each buys rewards exactly the wrong thing.** The honest comparison is assumptions needed to reach the same place with `1/R` **derived**; on that measure no run competes, because none derived it. **Three findings about ED came out of a construction task, and two are verified.** (1) **P12 as stated carries no other-chain dependence** and cannot express a two-body interaction — checked against `Paper_087` §P12, which defines all three terms as *the chain's* own content. **Correct as stated.** (2) **V5's definition presupposes Lorentz invariance the primitives do not establish.** (3) All three noted **P12 is already force-law-shaped**, so deriving gravitation from it is partly circular. **Both (1) and (2) are now Foundations staleness #5 and #6.** They are the arm's most valuable output and are not what it was testing for — the audit-mode strength showing up inside a construction run, exactly as the standing verdict predicts. **Protocol checks pass.** **F3 (anti-manufacturing) holds on all three** — GPT: *“claiming a derivation of gravitation would manufacture the missing physics”*; Claude: *“I did not find a legitimate derivation path I was withholding.”* No re-prompting, single paste each, no hints. **Conflict of interest declared in the write-up:** one family is Claude and the scorer is Claude; that family scored closest on T2 and produced the sharpest criticisms. T2 scoring is mechanical (derived vs postulated) and it fails on its own explicit statement, so the verdict is unaffected — but it is recorded rather than left to be noticed. **Arm B is now the live question and worth building.** The 13 **demonstrably** underdetermine ED; whether the **38** close the gap is exactly what remains unknown. Arm B seeds `P-RB-1` and the acoustic-metric guardrails, which bear directly on the metric-emergence gap **all three runs independently identified as the missing bridge** — so it has a real chance of moving, and a pass would make *“the 38 lines build ED”* a demonstrated claim rather than a framing. Full scoring: `substrate-evaluation/Result_F1_ArmA_2026-09-05.md`.
57. **The four-band question SETTLED 2026-09-05 — the partition is canonical; ≦56 withdrawn; the error is a July ledger entry, not the ten files.** **SETTLED: the four-band partition is canonical. ≦56's finding is withdrawn, and the real error is a July ledger entry that has been wrong since it was written.** `ARCHIVED_M_SERIES_NOTICE.md` archives the M-series — `paper_M0`–`M4`, the omnibus, `NPP_M1` — and **explicitly retains** ***“The Forcing Papers (#1–#19) with their §3.0 entries revised to honest postulational framing”*** as the program's current vehicle. **“Paper #3” is a Forcing Paper, not an M-paper.** **And `paper_M2` settles it from the other side, in its own abstract:** ED *“**is committed at the primitive level** to a four-band partition of bandwidth (P04 §1.5)”*. The partition is M2's **subject** and **pre-existed it**; M2's title is *“Primitive P04 — The Four-Band Partition — **is FORCED**”*, and it positions itself *“upstream of Forcing Papers #1–#19”*. **What the archive removed is the claim that the partition is forced from a deeper layer. The partition itself is untouched.** The distinction is exactly the pivot the notice describes: the 13 primitives are **posited**, and the M-series died trying to derive them. **So the actual defect is `Foundations` staleness #2, dated 2026-07-29.** It read *“Paper #3 — four-band orthogonality”* as an archived M-series citation, **repointed two `Paper_089` dependency lines away from a sound source**, and asserted a parenthetical — *“canonical 087 has no four-band”* — that conflates **‘087's summary entry does not enumerate the bands’** with **‘the bands are not canonical’**. That entry has stood unchallenged for five weeks and is what sent me down this path. **Corrected in place, with the repoint flagged for review.** **Four flags placed an hour ago are withdrawn**, with the superseded text kept as the audit trail in each: `Paper_027_Newtons_G`, `Paper_003_BornRule`, `primitives/P04_bandwidth.md`, and `soft-matter/Paper_UnifiedP04TransportBudget`. **The soft-matter arc's Prediction rests on a correct `P (primitive)` tier and needs no re-grounding** — ≦56's one finding is void, and the arc is clean without qualification. **The one genuine repair this leaves is upstream and small:** canonical `Paper_087` §P04 gives only the summary (*“bandwidth as non-negative additive scalar”*) and **does not cross-reference §1.5's partition**. That gap is what made the primitive card look like it contradicted the canonical paper, and it is what will make the next reader repeat this. **Owed:** add the cross-reference, and review whether `Paper_089`'s 2026-07-29 repoint should be reverted. **Recorded as a caution about my own method, because it nearly went the other way.** ≦56 correctly declined to propagate a stripping operation across ten files on the strength of one parenthetical — *“a large consequence to bank on a reading of one parenthetical in a July ledger entry”* — and that restraint is the only reason the corpus was not damaged. **The `CLAUDE.md` rule held: assume you misframed it, and read the source before acting.** The source here was two documents in a frozen fork that the flag-writing session had not opened. **Net for the day: this is the seventh propagation-failure-class finding and the first that inverts.** Five were corrections recorded and not distributed. ≦56 looked like a sixth. It was instead a **correction that should never have been made**, distributed exactly once — into `Paper_089` — and then cited as settled fact for five weeks. **A wrong fix propagates the same way a right one fails to.**

40. **Research 2026-09-04 — Prediction 1.16 registered: `H₀ = 2πa₀/c`, the Hubble constant from rotation curves. `Paper_ED_Pred_H0_From_a0.md` (new).** **The inversion nobody had written down.** `a₀ = cH₀/(2π)` read backwards is **`H₀ = 2πa₀/c` — a determination of the Hubble constant from galaxy rotation curves.** **⚠ CORRECTED 2026-09-05: the “no MOND analogue” claim was wrong.** The `a₀`–cosmology connection is discussed at length in MOND; Milgrom (2020), arXiv:2001.09729, is a review devoted to it. **What MOND lacks is a *derived normalization*** — it has `a₀ ∼ cH₀`, and an order-of-magnitude relation inverts only to an order-of-magnitude `H₀`, which measures nothing. **ED's distinctiveness is the coefficient, and the coefficient is the disputed part**, so the probe's novelty and the arc's weakest link are the same object. Arbitration needs `a₀` to **~4%** (±3 km/s/Mpc) or **2.6%** (±2); Desmond is at ~8%, so **a factor of two**, and the systematic there is mass-to-light normalisation rather than anything irreducible. **The virtue that makes it worth registering: independent systematics.** `a₀`'s error budget is dominated by stellar population synthesis and disk/bulge M/L. That shares nothing with the CMB sound horizon or with Cepheid/TRGB ladders. **A third probe in a two-camp tension is worth most when its errors are uncorrelated with both**, and this one's are. **Guarded against the obvious misuse.** One equation, two undetermined quantities (`k` and `H₀`), so it is a **joint constraint** usable in one direction at a time. Assume `k = 2π` → it measures `H₀`, and the result is a *test* of `k`. Assume `H₀` → it measures `k` (currently `5.67 ± 0.47` at `H₀ = 70` against `2π = 6.283`). **Measuring `H₀` from it and citing the agreement as support for the `2π` is circular and the paper says so.** **Free with the flagship's data.** ML 1.15 already predicts `a₀(z) = cH(z)/(2π)` with exponent exactly 1, and `Paper_029` §2.5 row 14 records that the exponent is **coefficient-independent**. So one redshift survey of the RAR delivers both: the **shape** tests the exponent, the **normalisation** gives this. The second half had not been written down. **Registered 2026-09-04, deliberately before the precision to test it exists**, so a later agreement is a prediction rather than a postdiction — the corpus's standing caution on that applies with full force. **Tier: M3, conditional on a coefficient that remains Postulated/disputed.** Nothing here derives the `2π`.
39. **Research 2026-09-04 — the `a₀` currency check run: the systematic tightened 2.7×, the `2π` is at 1.1σ not 0.5σ, and it prefers a high `H₀`. `Paper_029` §5.5 (new).** **The check named in ≦38 was run, and the ground had moved.** §5.4 was written against **McGaugh, Lelli & Schombert 2016**, `a₀ = 1.20 ± 0.02 (stat) ± 0.24 (sys)` — the ±0.24 being a 20% normalisation uncertainty from holding disk and bulge mass-to-light ratios fixed across SPARC. The current determination is **Desmond 2023** (MNRAS 526, 3342), an HMC joint inference that fits galaxy parameters simultaneously: **`a₀ = 1.19 ± 0.04 (stat) ± 0.09 (sys)`**. **The systematic has tightened by 2.7×.** **So §5.4's wording is stale, as ≦38 suspected but could not show.** ED's `2π` requires `a₀ = 1.082` at `H₀ = 70`. Against ±0.241 that is **0.49σ** — *“consistent”* was fair. Against ±0.098 it is **1.09σ**, and the offset is one-sided at every `H₀` in the range. The honest reading is now **not excluded at ~1.1σ**, not *consistent*. §5.4's note is replaced by a new **§5.5** carrying both determinations, the σ-table, and the falsifier below. **The substantive result: `a₀ = cH₀/(2π)` takes a side in the Hubble tension.** Required `a₀` runs 1.042 (Planck `H₀ = 67.4`) to 1.144 (`H₀ = 74`), so against Desmond the offset is **1.50σ at Planck and 0.62σ at SH0ES**. **The relation is markedly more comfortable with the local distance-ladder `H₀` than with the CMB value.** *This is not a prediction of `H₀`* — `Paper_029` §8.1 disclaims deriving it, and ED inherits both numbers — **it is a consistency constraint between two independently measured quantities**, and it is the sharpest empirical handle the `2π` has ever had. **The `a₀` side is robust against the obvious objection.** A fitted `a₀` might be thought to depend on the assumed `μ` family, which would blunt the test. It does not: `a₀` is *defined* by the deep-MOND normalization and the asymptotes cross at `a₀` for every family (threshold note §5, checked numerically across three). Desmond marginalises over functional forms and lands within 0.01 of the 2016 fixed-form value — which is exactly what that argument predicts, and is a small independent confirmation of it. **New falsifier F-2π, and it sharpens without anyone doing theory.** If `a₀` tightens further while the CMB `H₀` holds, the `2π` is squeezed structurally-untouched: at `H₀ = 67.4` a combined `±0.05` puts the offset at **3σ**.
